%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%% 0000013045000160  
%            ALGOL ACCESS TO POSIX PROCEDURES                           0000013145000180  
%                                                                       0000013245000200  
% This ALGOL include file provides POSIX library procedure              0000013345000220  
% declarations for the POSIX MCPSUPPORT library interfaces.  This file  0000013445000240  
% can be included in ALGOL programs.  Programmers can access the new    0000013545000260  
% procedures using the names defined in this file instead of the actual 0000013645000280  
% names.  The names are chosen to mimic the "C" POSIX API calls.        0000013745000300  
% Additional symbols are defined to give more flexibility to the user   0000013845000320  
% for setting flags or values.                                          0000013945000340  
%                                                                       0000014045000360  
%  This file is organized in the following fashion:                     0000014145000380  
%                                                                       0000014245000400  
%  o  Part 1 of the file contains the interface declarations and        0000014345000420  
%     selector values                                                   0000014445000430  
%     1.   signal related calls                                         0000014545000440  
%     2.   semaphore related calls                                      0000014645000460  
%     3.   user/group info related calls                                0000014745000480  
%     4.   selector values are defined to symbols                       0000014845000490  
%                                                                       0000014945000500  
%  o  Part 2 of the file contains the macro definitions                 0000015045000520  
%     1.   errno values are defined to symbols                          0000015145000540  
%     2.   other flags are defined to symbols                           0000015245000580  
%                                                                       0000015345000600  
%  o  Part 3 of the file contains the re-definition of procedure        0000015445000620  
%     calls to simulate "C" interface call names.                       0000015545000640  
%                                                                       0000015645000660  
%  It is recommended that user access to these procedures be            0000015745000680  
%  made using the constants in PART 2 and the interfaces in PART 3.     0000015845000700  
%                                                                       0000015945000720  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%% 0000016045000740  
%                                                                       0000016145000760  
%                                                                       0000016245000780  
%  MCPSUPPORT Library Interfaces for POSIX                              0000016345000800  
%                                                                       0000016445000820  
%  General Definition Rules                                             0000016545000840  
%  ========================                                             0000016645000860  
%  1. All MCP interfaces which take pointers to character strings or    0000016745000880  
%     structures as input parameters will also take a by-value integer  0000016845000900  
%     string or structure length as a separate parameter.  The appli-   0000016945000920  
%     cation is responsible for providing this length. The MCP routines 0000017045000940  
%     will rely on this length information to determine how much of the 0000017145000960  
%     string or structure information is valid.                         0000017245000980  
%                                                                       0000017345001000  
%     In the case of strings, if the length is -1, the called function  0000017445001020  
%     will assume that the string is NULL terminated, and act according-0000017545001040  
%     ly.  If a string length is provided, it is assumed to NOT include 0000017645001060  
%     any trailing NULL.                                                0000017745001080  
%                                                                       0000017845001100  
%     All input structures with embedded strings will include an integer0000017945001120  
%     preceding the start of the string.  This integer will contain the 0000018045001140  
%     length of the string. If the length is -1, the MCP will assume    0000018145001160  
%     that the string is NULL terminated; if the length is not -1, it   0000018245001180  
%     will be taken as the length of the string,  not including the     0000018345001200  
%     NULL.                                                             0000018445001220  
%                                                                       0000018545001240  
%  2. All MCP interfaces which take pointers to character strings or    0000018645001260  
%     structures as output parameters will also return the length of the0000018745001280  
%     string or structure in a by-reference integer output parameter    0000018845001300  
%     unless such information can be returned as the interface result.  0000018945001320  
%     (See 5. for more details.)                                        0000019045001340  
%     Additionally, a by-value integer maximum string or structure      0000019145001360  
%     length input parameter must be provided.  The MCP interfaces      0000019245001380  
%     require a valid string or structure length maximum value, since   0000019345001400  
%     this length in conjunction with the actual size of the string or  0000019445001420  
%     structure container will determine how much of the string or      0000019545001440  
%     structure information will be written.                            0000019645001460  
%                                                                       0000019745001480  
%     The MCP interfaces will place a terminating NULL at the end of an 0000019845001500  
%     output character string if there is room and the returned length  0000019945001520  
%     will not include the NULL character.                              0000020045001540  
%                                                                       0000020145001560  
%     No error will be returned if there is no space for a NULL         0000020245001580  
%     character at the end of the data. However if there is insufficient0000020345001600  
%     room for the data, then EINVAL will be returned in the            0000020445001620  
%     by-reference integer output parameter ERRNO and unexpected result 0000020545001640  
%     will be returned. The documentation for each interface specifies  0000020645001660  
%     the behavior for all possible conditions.                         0000020745001700  
%                                                                       0000020845001720  
%     All output structures with embedded strings will include an       0000020945001740  
%     integer preceding the start of the string.  This integer will     0000021045001760  
%     contain the length of the string, excluding the NULL; overflow    0000021145001780  
%     conditions will be handled as described above.                    0000021245001800  
%                                                                       0000021345001820  
%  3. The application is responsible for guaranteeing the alignment of  0000021445001840  
%     embedded word numeric types (i.e.  integers) within structures for0000021545001860  
%     all MCP interfaces which take pointers to structures.  All        0000021645001880  
%     structures must start on a word boundary.                         0000021745001900  
%                                                                       0000021845001920  
%  4. For MCP interfaces with Selector parameters, any parameter (input 0000021945001940  
%     or output) is undefined if not used for that selector value. The  0000022045001960  
%     documentation for each interface states which parameters are      0000022145001980  
%     valid and which are undefined for each selector value.            0000022245002000  
%                                                                       0000022345002020  
%  5. All MCP interfaces return an integer or real result.  The result, 0000022445002040  
%     -1, indicates that an error has occurred and that ERRNO has       0000022545002060  
%     probably been modified.  A meaningful positive or negative result 0000022645002080  
%     may also be returned if specified by POSIX. MCP interfaces        0000022745002100  
%     never return a pointer result; however, if the POSIX              0000022845002120  
%     interface either returns a pointer, or an output pointer is its   0000022945002140  
%     only output parameter AND the function returns no other meaningful0000023045002160  
%     result, then the results of such functions will be the length of  0000023145002180  
%     the string supplied by the MCP.                                   0000023245002200  
%                                                                       0000023345002220  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%% 0000023445002240  
$$PAGE                                                                  0000023545003000  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%                                                   0000023645003020  
%    PART 1         %                                                   0000023745003040  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%                                                   0000023845003060

% CMD values and associated parameters are as follows:                  0000152345607500  
   F_DUPFD  = 0 #, % Only supported for POSIX Global Files.  Causes file0000152445607520  
                   % descriptor to be copied into the first available   0000152545607540  
                   % file descriptor vector table entry.                0000152645607560

                   % specified by the flock structure.                  0000153445607720

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%    0000153845607800  
%       POSIX flock STRUCTURE (POSIX_FCNTL)                        %    0000153945607820  
%..................................................................%    0000154045607840  
% The flock structure is used to specify locks for POSIX defined   %    0000154145607860  
% advisory locking.  Advisory locking is supported by POSIX_FCNTL. %    0000154245607880  
% POSIX_FCNTL uses FILELOCKER which also provides record locking   %    0000154345607900  
% for general A Series use.  The words in the flock structure map  %    0000154445607920  
% to FILELOCKER pararmeters.                                       %    0000154545607940  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%    0000154645607960  
    %-Word-offset-- %--Description--------------  %--FILELOCKER params  0000154745607980  
    L_TYPE   = 0 #, % Type of lock.               % TYPE                0000154845608000  
    L_WHENCE = 1 #, % Where L_START is based.     % WHENCE              0000154945608020  
    L_START  = 2 #, % Relative offset in bytes.   % START  RETURNSTART  0000155045608040  
    L_LEN    = 3 #, % Length of area in bytes.    % LEN    RETURNLEN    0000155145608060  
    L_PID    = 4 #, % Process ID of lock holder.  % n/a.   RETURNPID    0000155245608080  
    L_PAD1   = 5 #, % For future growth.          % n/a.                0000155345608100  
    FLOCK_LEN= 6 #; % Length of "flock" structure                       0000155445608120  
                                                                        0000155545608140  
   DEFINE                                                               0000155645608160  
   %%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%                                            0000155745608180  
   % Type of lock (L_TYPE) %                                            0000155845608200  
   %%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%                                            0000155945608220  
      F_RDLCK   =  1 #, % reader lock                                   0000156045608240  
      F_WRLCK   =  2 #, % writer lock                                   0000156145608260  
      F_UNLCK   =  3 #; % unlock (or not locked)                        0000156245608280  
                                                                        0000156345608300  
   DEFINE                                                               0000156445608320  
   %%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%                                            0000156545608340  
   %      L_WHENCE         %                                            0000156645608360  
   %%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%                                            0000156745608380  
      SEEK_SET  =  0 #, % apply offset from beginning of file.          0000156845608400  
      SEEK_CUR  =  1 #, % apply offset from current file pointer pos.   0000156945608420  
      SEEK_END  =  2 #; % apply offset from EOF (byte past last written)0000157045608440  
                                                                        0000157145608460  
   DEFINE                                                               0000157245608480

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%0000187046100050  
%   ACCESS: This procedure allows the caller to determine if the        0000187146100060  
%           permissions specified via AMODE are available for the file  0000187246100070  
%           specified by PATH.                                          0000187346100080  
%                                                                       0000187446100090  
%   INPUT:  For the description of PATH, PATH_OFF, PATH_LEN, PATH_TYPE, 0000187546100100  
%           and PATH_SEARCHRULE, refer to the POSIX_ACCESS Pathname     0000187646100110  
%           defines in this file.                                       0000187746100120  
%           AMODE (integer) - Specifies Bitwise inclusive OR of the     0000187846100130  
%                 following set of permissions:                         0000187946100140  
%                                                                       0000188046100150  
%               NAME/VALUE   BIT   Permission                           0000188146100160  
%               ----------  -----  ----------                           0000188246100170  
%               R_OK = 4   [2:1]  Read                                  0000188346100180  
%               W_OK = 2   [1:1]  Write                                 0000188446100190  
%               X_OK = 1   [0:1]  Execute/Search                        0000188546100200  
%               F_OK = 0    ---   Existence (always checked)            0000188646100210  
%                                                                       0000188746100220  
%   OUTPUT: none                                                        0000188846100230  
%                                                                       0000188946100240  
%   RESULT:                                                             0000189046100250  
%     If an error occurs, ERRNO is set and a -1 result is returned.     0000189146100260  
%     Otherwise 0 is returned indicating the file exists and the        0000189246100270  
%     caller has the access permissions specified by AMODE.             0000189346100280  
%     If the specified file name is shared by both a file and a         0000189446100290  
%     directory, access will be checked for the directory.              0000189546100300  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%0000189646100310  
                                                                        0000189746100320

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%% 0000200046216200  
%   EXECVE:                                                             0000200146216210  
%     This procedure handles all the user exec interfaces.              0000200246216220  
%                                                                       0000200346216230  
%   INPUT:  For the description of PATH, PATH_OFF, PATH_LEN, PATH_TYPE, 0000200446216240  
%           and PATH_SEARCHRULE, refer to the POSIX_ACCESS Pathname     0000200546216250  
%           defines in this file.                                       0000200646216260  
%     ARGV - an ebcdic array containing parameters to be passed to the  0000200746216270  
%        program being execed.                                          0000200846216280  
%     ARGV_OFF (integer) - not used (only exist for consistency).       0000200946216290  
%     ARGV_LEN (integer) - not used (only exist for consistency).       0000201046216300  
%     ARGVINDX - an integer array of indexes into ARGV which point to   0000201146216310  
%        the start of each argument.                                    0000201246216320  
%     ARGVINDX_OFF (integer) - offset into ARGVINDX of where the        0000201346216330  
%        indexes start.                                                 0000201446216340  
%     ARGVINDX_LEN (integer) - number of parameters in ARGV             0000201546216350  
%        (number of indexes).                                           0000201646216360  
%     ARGVSZ - an integer array containing to length of each parameter  0000201746216370  
%        in ARGV pointed to by the index found in ARGVINDX.             0000201846216380  
%     ARGVSZ_OFF (integer) - offset into ARGVSZ of where the sizes      0000201946216390  
%        are stored.                                                    0000202046216400  
%     ARGVSZ_LEN (integer) - number of parameters in ARGV (number       0000202146216410  
%        of sizes). Must be equal to ARGVINDX_LEN.                      0000202246216420  
%     ENVP - an ebcdic array containing environment parameters to be    0000202346216430  
%        passed to the program being execed.                            0000202446216440  
%     ENVP_OFF (integer) - not used (only exist for consistency).       0000202546216450  
%     ENVP_LEN (integer) - not used (only exist for consistency).       0000202646216460  
%     ENVPINDX - an integer array of indexes into ENVP which point to   0000202746216470  
%        the start of each environment parameter.                       0000202846216480  
%     ENVPINDX_OFF (integer) - offset into ENVPINDX of where the indexes0000202946216490  
%        start.                                                         0000203046216500  
%     ENVPINDX_LEN (integer) - number of environment parameters in ENVP 0000203146216510  
%        (number of indexes).                                           0000203246216520  
%     ENVPSZ - an integer array containing to length of each environment0000203346216530  
%        parameter in ENVP pointed to by the index found in ENVPINDX.   0000203446216540

%        stored.                                                        0000203646216560  
%     ENVPSZ_LEN (integer) - number of parameters in ENVP (number of    0000203746216570

%                                                                       0000203946216590  
%   OUTPUT: none                                                        0000204046216600

DEFINE    SEMCTL(SEMID, SEMNUM, CMD, VAL,                               0000258246450000  
                    ARG, ARG_OFF, ARG_LEN, ERRNO)   =                   0000258346450020

                        ARG, ARG_OFF, ARG_LEN, ERRNO) #;                0000258546450060  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%0000258646450080  
%   SEMCTL: provides a set of semaphore control operations as           0000258746450100  
%           specified by the CMD parameter.                             0000258846450120  
%                                                                       0000258946450140  
%   INPUT: SEMID (integer) - semaphore id for a set                     0000259046450160  
%          SEMNUM (integer) - semaphore member number within a set      0000259146450180  
%          CMD (integer) - action to be performed                       0000259246450200  
%              IPC_STAT    %read semaphore values of the semaphore set  0000259346450220  
%              IPC_SET     %set semaphore values of the semaphore set   0000259446450240  
%              IPC_RMID    %remove semaphore id from the system         0000259546450260  
%              SEM_GETNCNT %get # procs waiting for a certain sem value 0000259646450280  
%              SEM_GETPID  %get id of last process to operate on sem set0000259746450300  
%              SEM_GETVAL  %get the semaphore value                     0000259846450320  
%              SEM_GETALL  %get all semaphore values in the set         0000259946450340  
%              SEM_GETZCNT %get # procs waiting for sem value to be 0   0000260046450360  
%              SEM_SETVAL  %set a semaphore value                       0000260146450380  
%              SEM_SETALL  %set all semaphore value in the set          0000260246450400  
%          VAL (integer) - semaphore value for the SEM_SETVAL command   0000260346450420  
%          ARG (real array) - contains the following information:       0000260446450440  
%              If CMD = SEM_GETALL or SEM_SETALL, ARG contains every    0000260546450460  
%                 semvals in the set of semaphores.                     0000260646450480  
%              If CMD = IPC_STAT or IPC_SET, then ARG contains          0000260746450500  
%                 semid_ds structure. For more information about this   0000260846450520  
%                 structure, refer to the Macro definition section      0000260946450540  
%                 of this include file.                                 0000261046450560  
%          ARG_OFF (integer) - word offset into the array ARG           0000261146450580  
%          ARG_LEN (integer) -  size of a semid_ds structure            0000261246450600  
%                                                                       0000261346450620  
%   OUTPUT:                                                             0000261446450640  
%          ARG (integer array) - holds a semid_ds structure for the     0000261546450660  
%                 IPC_STAT and IPC_SET command. For more info about     0000261646450680  
%                 this structure, refer to the procedure SEMCTL.        0000261746450700  
%                                                                       0000261846450760  
%   RETURN: Upon successful completion, the procedure returns the       0000261946450780  
%           non-zero integer value for SEM_GETVAL, SEM_GETPID,          0000262046450800  
%           SEM_GETNCNT, and SEM_GETZCNT command.                       0000262146450820  
%           For other commands, the procedure returns zero.             0000262246450840  
%           If an error occurs, the procedure returns -1 and ERRNO is   0000262346450860  
%           set to indicate the error.                                  0000262446450880  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%0000262546450900  
                                                                        0000262646450920  
                                                                        0000262746450940

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%0000291646660120  
%   SIGACTION: allows the calling process to examine and/or specify     0000291746660140  
%              the action to be associated with a specific signal.      0000291846660160  
%                                                                       0000291946660180  
%   INPUT: SIG (integer) - signal number (refer to the sig values       0000292046660200  
%                          defined in this file)                        0000292146660220  
%          ACT_PROC - signal handling procedure to be invoked when a    0000292246660240  
%                     specified signal has been occurred. This procedure0000292346660260  
%                     must be declared as an integer procedure and take 0000292446660280  
%                     ten integer variables.                            0000292546660300  
%                                                                       0000292646660320  
%                INPUT:                                                 0000292746660340  
%                   INFO1 - version and signal number                   0000292846660360  
%                   INFO2 - sa_handler                                  0000292946660380  
%                   INFO3 - additional signal state information         0000293046660400  
%                   INFO4 - pid for si_pid member of siginfo structure  0000293146660420  
%                   INFO5 - uid for si_uid member of siginfo structure  0000293246660440  
%                   INFO6 - signal value for si_value member of the     0000293346660460  
%                           siginfo structure                           0000293446660480  
%                   INFO7 - for future expansion                        0000293546660500  
%                   INFO8 - for future expansion                        0000293646660520  
%                   INFO9 - for future expansion                        0000293746660540  
%                   INFO10 - for future expansion                       0000293846660560  
%                RETURN:                                                0000293946660580  
%                   result not used                                     0000294046660600  
%                                                                       0000294146660620  
%          ACT (real array) - the sigaction structure                   0000294246660640  
%              This structure contains the following 3 words:           0000294346660660  
%                sa_handler - SIG_FDFL, SIG_IGN or a unique number      0000294446660680  
%                             which is passed as the INFO2 parameter    0000294546660700  
%                             to the ACT_PROC procedure.                0000294646660720  
%                sa_mask    - a set of signals to be blocked during the 0000294746660740  
%                             execution of the signal-catching function 0000294846660760  
%                sa_flag    - flags which affect the behavior of signal 0000294946660780  
%                                                                       0000295046660800  
%          ACT_OFF (integer) - word offset into the array ACT           0000295146660820  
%                                                                       0000295246660840  
%          ACT_LEN (integer) -  the length of the sigaction structure   0000295346660860  
%              If the length is 0, then the ACT is a null structure.    0000295446660880  
%                                                                       0000295546660900  
%          OACT_OFF (integer) - word offset into the ACT structure      0000295646660920  
%          OACT_MAX (integer) - the length of the sigaction structure   0000295746660940  
%               If length is 0, then the ACT is a null structure.       0000295846660960  
%   OUTPUT:                                                             0000295946660980  
%          OACT (real array) - the sigaction structure                  0000296046661000

%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%% 0000328646830060  
%   STAT: Returns the file status associated with the file named in     0000328746830070  
%          the PATH.                                                    0000328846830080  
%                                                                       0000328946830090  
%   INPUT:  For the description of PATH, PATH_OFF, PATH_LEN, PATH_TYPE, 0000329046830100  
%           and PATH_SEARCHRULE, refer to the POSIX_ACCESS Pathname     0000329146830110  
%           defines in this file.                                       0000329246830120  
%           BUF_OFF (integer) - the word offset into the array BUF      0000329346830130  
%           BUF_MAX (integer) - max size in words starting from BUF_OFF 0000329446830140  
%                         to be filled in                               0000329546830150  
%                                                                       0000329646830160  
%   OUTPUT: BUF (real array) - holds the file status structure.         0000329746830170  
%           Refer to the Part 2 of this file for the definition of the  0000329846830180  
%           file status structure.                                      0000329946830190  
%                                                                       0000330046830200  
%   RETURN: Upon successful completion, the procedure returns the       0000330146830210  
%           file status structure in the array BUF.                     0000330246830220  
%           If BUF_MAX does not have enough space to hold the file      0000330346830230  
%           status structure, then it will be truncated.                0000330446830240  
%           If an error occurs, the procedure returns -1 and ERRNO is   0000330546830250  
%           set to indicate the error.                                  0000330646830260  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%% 0000330746830270  
                                                                        0000330846830280  
INTEGER PROCEDURE STRERROR(BUFF, BUFF_OFF, BUFF_MAX, ERRNO);            0000330946850080  
VALUE                            BUFF_OFF, BUFF_MAX, ERRNO;             0000331046850100  
REFERENCE                  BUFF;                                        0000331146850120  
EBCDIC ARRAY               BUFF[0];                                     0000331246850140  
INTEGER                          BUFF_OFF, BUFF_MAX, ERRNO;             0000331346850160  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%0000331446850180  
%   STRERROR: converts an error number to a string and returns the      0000331546850200  
%             string in the return argument.                            0000331646850220  
%                                                                       0000331746850240  
%   INPUT: ERRNO (integer) - error number                               0000331846850260  
%                                                                       0000331946850280  
%   OUTPUT: BUFF (EBCDIC array) - place holder for an error message     0000332046850300  
%           BUFF_OFF (integer) - byte offset into the array BUFF        0000332146850320  
%           BUFF_MAX (integer) - maximum length of the array BUFF       0000332246850340  
%                                                                       0000332346850360  
%   RETURN: length of string or -1 if an error occurred.                0000332446850380  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%0000332546850400  
BEGIN                                                                   0000332646850420  
   EBCDIC ARRAY EA[0:POSIX_ERR_ARRAY_SIZE-1];                           0000332746850440  
   INTEGER L;                                                           0000332846850460  
                                                                        0000332946850480  
   C_STRERROR(ERRNO, EA[0], FALSE);                                     0000333046850500  
   IF ERRNO >=  0 THEN                                                  0000333146850520  
   BEGIN                                                                0000333246850540  
      SCAN EA[0] FOR L:POSIX_ERR_ARRAY_SIZE UNTIL = 0;                  0000333346850560  
      L:=POSIX_ERR_ARRAY_SIZE-L;                                        0000333446850580  
      REPLACE BUFF[BUFF_OFF] BY EA[0] FOR MIN(BUFF_MAX,L);              0000333546850600  
      STRERROR := MIN(BUFF_MAX, L);                                     0000333646850610  
   END ELSE BEGIN                                                       0000333746850620  
      STRERROR := -1;                                                   0000333846850630  
   END;                                                                 0000333946850640  
END OF STRERROR;                                                        0000334046850650  
                                                                        0000334146850660  
DEFINE SVFORK(ERRNO) =                                                  0000334246850670  
       POSIX_FORK(SVFORKV, ERRNO) #;                                    0000334346850680  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%% 0000334446850690  
%   FORK:                                                               0000334546850700  
%        This procedure creates a clone of the entire calling process   0000334646850710  
%        by executing the top frame only.  Use it if you are going to   0000334746850720  
%        call exec() right away.  The parent thread is suspended        0000334846850730  
%        until the child() does an exec() or _exit().  After an exec(), 0000334946850740  
%        but before any user code is executed, the child waits on an MCP0000335046850750  
%        event.  The parent thread must call svfork_release(<child pid>)0000335146850760  
%        to allow the child to continue.  This gives the parent time    0000335246850770  
%        to enter information about the child before the child can      0000335346850780  
%        access it or exit().                                           0000335446850790  
%                                                                       0000335546850800  
%   INPUT: none                                                         0000335646850810  
%                                                                       0000335746850820  
%   OUTPUT: none                                                        0000335846850830  
%                                                                       0000335946850840  
%   RETURN: Upon successful completion, the procedure returns 0.        0000336046850850  
%           If an error occurs, the procedure returns -1 and ERRNO is   0000336146850860  
%           set to indicate the error.                                  0000336246850870  
%           If the function succeeds, a new process will be created that0000336346850880  
%           will be returned a zero;  the calling process will be       0000336446850890  
%           returned the MIX number of the new process.                 0000336546850900  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%% 0000336646850910  
                                                                        0000336746850920  
DEFINE SVFORK_RELEASE(PID, ERRNO) =                                     0000336846850925  
       POSIX_SETIDS(RELEASEVFORK_SV, PID, 0, ERRNO) #;                  0000336946850930  
%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%%% 0000337046850935  
%   FORK:                                                               0000337146850940

 PROCEDURE OBTEN_EVE(MIX_LOCK,LLAMADOR);                                0000409300756032  
 VALUE     MIX_LOCK,LLAMADOR;                                           0000409400756052  
 INTEGER   MIX_LOCK,LLAMADOR;                                           0000409500756092  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000409600756112  
    EBCDIC ARRAY  RUTINA[0:20];                                         0000409700756122  
    REPLACE RUTINA BY 48"00" FOR 21;                                    0000409800756127  
    TOTAL := 0;                                                         0000409900756132  
    CASE LLAMADOR OF                                                    0000410000756134  
    BEGIN                                                               0000410100756136  
       0 : REPLACE RUTINA BY " respuesta de Tandem ";                   0000410200756138  
       1 : REPLACE RUTINA BY " create ";                                0000410300756140  
       2 : REPLACE RUTINA BY " envio ";                                 0000410400756142  
       3 : REPLACE RUTINA BY " reenvio ";                               0000410500756144  
    ELSE : REPLACE RUTINA BY " NO CONOCIDO ";                           0000410600756146  
    END;                                                                0000410700756148  
    RES_LOCK := LOCK(ACT_BIFIN,TIEMPO);                                 0000410800756152  
    WHILE RES_LOCK > 1 DO                                               0000410900756172  
    BEGIN                                                               0000411000756192  
       TOTAL := * + TIEMPO;                                             0000411100756212  
       IF TOTAL > 15 THEN                                               0000411200756232  
       BEGIN                                                            0000411300756252  
          REPLACE MSG_ERR BY 48"00" FOR 227               ;             0000411400756262  
          REPLACE MSG_ERR BY                                            0000411500756272  
                  "Evento atrapado por "                  ,             0000411600756292  
                  RUTINA UNTIL = 48"00"                   ,             0000411700756302  
                  " # MIX "                               ,             0000411800756307  
                  MIX_LOCK_ANT FOR 5 DIGITS               ,             0000411900756312  
                  " llevo "                               ,             0000412000756332  
                  TOTAL FOR 4 DIGITS                      ,             0000412100756352  
                  " segundos en espera"                   ;             0000412200756372  
          IF SALIDA.OPEN THEN WRITE(SALIDA,40,MSG_ERR)    ;             0000412300756392  
       END;                                                             0000412400756412  
       RES_LOCK := LOCK(ACT_BIFIN,TIEMPO);                              0000412500756432  
    END;                                                                0000412600756452  
    MIX_LOCK_ANT := MIX_LOCK;                                           0000412700756552  
  END;                                                                  0000412800756652

  INTEGER PROCEDURE FUNCIONL12(DATOS);                                  0000414100757012  
  EBCDIC ARRAY DATOS[*];                                                0000414200758012  
  BEGIN                                                                 0000414300759012  
    DEFINE IDFFUN_L002(A) = INTEGER(POINTER(A), 2)#; % 02               0000414400760012  
    ARRAY   ENTRADA[0:5];                                               0000414500761012  
    EBCDIC  ARRAY NOM_ASINC[0:50];                                      0000414600762012  
    INTEGER I, EVEN;                                                    0000414700763012  
                                                                        0000414800764012  
%===============================================================        0000414900765012  
% INICIA CODIGO DE PROCEDIMIENTO FUNCIONL12                             0000415000766012  
%===============================================================        0000415100767012  
    REPLACE ENTRADA BY DATOS FOR 2;                                     0000415200768012  
    CASE IDFFUN_L002(ENTRADA) OF                                        0000415300769012  
    BEGIN                                                               0000415400770012  
        1 : BEGIN                                                       0000415500771012  
               REPLACE MSGLJ BY STR_HDRMSG, 48"00" FOR 300;             0000415600771212  
               REPLACE TXTMSG BY "FECHA PROCESO      "      ,           0000415700771312  
                                 FECHA_PROCESO FOR 8 DIGITS ;           0000415800771317  
               LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                         0000415900771319  
               REPLACE MSGLJ BY STR_HDRMSG, 48"00" FOR 300;             0000416000771322  
               REPLACE TXTMSG BY "TRANS. POR SEGUNDO "      ,           0000416100771337  
                                 TRANS_X_SEG    FOR 8 DIGITS;           0000416200771342  
               LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                         0000416300771344  
               REPLACE MSGLJ BY STR_HDRMSG, 48"00" FOR 300;             0000416400771347  
               REPLACE TXTMSG BY "ESPERA ENTRE ENVIOS "     ,           0000416500771362  
                                 TMPO_ESPERA    FOR 6 DIGITS;           0000416600771367  
               LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                         0000416700771369  
               REPLACE MSGLJ BY STR_HDRMSG, 48"00" FOR 300;             0000416800771372  
               REPLACE TXTMSG BY "MOVTOS. LEIDOS     "      ,           0000416900771387  
                                 TOT_LEIDOS     FOR 8 DIGITS;           0000417000771392  
               LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                         0000417100771394  
               REPLACE MSGLJ BY STR_HDRMSG, 48"00" FOR 300;             0000417200771397  
               REPLACE TXTMSG BY "MOVTOS. ENVIADOS   "      ,           0000417300771437  
                                 TOT_ENVIADOS  FOR 8 DIGITS ;           0000417400771457  
               LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                         0000417500771467  
               REPLACE MSGLJ BY STR_HDRMSG, 48"00" FOR 300;             0000417600771477  
               REPLACE TXTMSG BY "MOVTOS. CONF. TANDEM. "   ,           0000417700771517  
                                 TOT_TANDEM    FOR 8 DIGITS ;           0000417800771537  
               LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                         0000417900771547  
               REPLACE MSGLJ BY STR_HDRMSG, 48"00" FOR 300;             0000418000771557  
               REPLACE TXTMSG BY "MOVTOS. RECHAZADOS "      ,           0000418100771597  
                                 TOT_ERRORES   FOR 8 DIGITS ;           0000418200771617  
               LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                         0000418300771627  
               REPLACE MSGLJ BY STR_HDRMSG, 48"00" FOR 300;             0000418400771637  
               REPLACE TXTMSG BY "RECH. X DUPLICADOS "      ,           0000418500771677  
                                 TOT_DUPLICADOS FOR 8 DIGITS;           0000418600771697  
               LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                         0000418700771707  
               REPLACE MSGLJ BY STR_HDRMSG, 48"00" FOR 300;             0000418800771717  
               REPLACE TXTMSG BY "MOVTOS. REENVIADOS "      ,           0000418900771757  
                                 TOT_REENVIOS  FOR 8 DIGITS ;           0000419000771777  
               LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                         0000419100771787  
               REPLACE MSGLJ BY STR_HDRMSG, 48"00" FOR 300;             0000419200771797  
               REPLACE TXTMSG BY "ERRORES EN ENVIOS  "      ,           0000419300771799  
                                 TOT_ERRORES_TCP FOR 8 DIGITS;          0000419400771800  
               LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                         0000419500771810  
               REPLACE MSGLJ BY STR_HDRMSG, 48"00" FOR 300;             0000419600771820  
               REPLACE TXTMSG BY "RESP CON ERROR TANDEM "   ,           0000419700771840  
                                 TOT_RESP_ERR_TANDEM FOR 8 DIGITS;      0000419800771860  
               LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                         0000419900771870  
               IF HI_11 OR DESLIGA_TCP THEN                             0000420000771925  
               BEGIN                                                    0000420100771930  
                  REPLACE MSGLJ BY STR_HDRMSG, 48"00" FOR 227;          0000420200771935  
                  REPLACE TXTMSG BY "TCP CARRIER DESLIGADA";            0000420300771940  
                  LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                      0000420400771945  
               END                                                      0000420500771950  
               ELSE                                                     0000420600771955  
               BEGIN                                                    0000420700771960  
                  REPLACE MSGLJ BY STR_HDRMSG, 48"00" FOR 227;          0000420800771965  
                  REPLACE TXTMSG BY "TCP CARRIER LIGADA";               0000420900771970  
                  LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                      0000421000771975  
               END;                                                     0000421100771980  
            END;                                                        0000421200771985  
        4 : BEGIN                                                       0000421300772012  
            IF MYSELF.LIBRARYUSERS = 0 THEN                             0000421400773012  
                BOOL_FIN := TRUE;    % BAJA DE LIBRERIA                 0000421500774012  
            END;                                                        0000421600775012  
        5: BEGIN                                                        0000421700775017  
              REPLACE DATOS[2] BY FECHA_PROCESO FOR 8 DIGITS,           0000421800775022  
                                  TOT_ENVIADOS  FOR 8 DIGITS,           0000421900775027  
                                  TOT_TANDEM    FOR 8 DIGITS;           0000422000775032  
           END;                                                         0000422100775062  
        17 : BEGIN  % DEFAULT PARA TIEMPO DE ESPERA Y BLOCK DE ENVIO    0000422200775112  
                TMPO_ESPERA  := 1;                                      0000422300775212  
                BLOCK_ENVIOS := 200;                                    0000422400775312  
             END;                                                       0000422500775412  
       25 : BEGIN                                                       0000422600776012  
               WHILE LIB_SBC DO WAIT((.01));                            0000422700776022  
               CAUSE(CAMBIA_DIA)        ;                               0000422800776032  
               CLOSE(MOVS_CDIR[0],LOCK) ;                               0000422900779012  
               CLOSE(MOVS_CDIR[1],LOCK) ;                               0000423000780012  
               SIST_LIB := SIST_LIB500  ;                               0000423100782012  
               EVEN := WAITANDRESET(((60)),LEE_ARCHIVO);                0000423200782022  
               IF EVEN = 1 THEN                                         0000423300782032  
               BEGIN                                                    0000423400782042  
                  REPLACE MSGLJ BY STR_HDRMSG, 48"00" FOR 227;          0000423500782052  
                  REPLACE TXTMSG BY " L012> S",                         0000423600782062  
                  SIST_LIB FOR 3 DIGITS,                                0000423700782072  
                  " NO SE REALIZO CAMBIO DE DIA";                       0000423800782082  
                  LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                      0000423900782092  
                  ACCEPT(TXTMSG);                                       0000424000782097  
               END                                                      0000424100782102  
               ELSE                                                     0000424200782112  
                  INICIA;                                               0000424300783012  
               CAUSE(LEE_ARCHIVO) ;                                     0000424400783022  
               RESET(CAMBIA_DIA)  ;                                     0000424500783112  
            END;                                                        0000424600784012  
       50 : BEGIN                                                       0000424700785012  
               FUNCION50 := FUNCION50 + 1;                              0000424800785112  
               IF FUNCION50 = 1 THEN                                    0000424900785212  
                  IF NOT SBC_LIBERADO THEN CAUSE(LIBERA_SBC);           0000425000786012  
               A:= 1 ;                                                  0000425100787012  
            END;                                                        0000425200788012  
       98 : BEGIN                                                       0000425300789012  
               CLOSE(MOVS_CDIR[0],LOCK);                                0000425400790012  
               CLOSE(MOVS_CDIR[1],LOCK);                                0000425500791012  
            END;                                                        0000425600793012  
       ELSE : ;                                                         0000425700794012  
    END;      % FIN CASE                                                0000425800795012  
    FUNCIONL12 := 0;                                                    0000425900796012  
  END OF FUNCIONL12;                                                    0000426000797012  
                                                                        0000426100798012  
  PROCEDURE BAJA;                                                       0000426200799012  
  BEGIN                                                                 0000426300800012  
     INTEGER RESPUESTA;                                                 0000426400801012  
     RESPUESTA := LINKLIBRARY(CONTROL);                                 0000426500802012  
     IF BD_ABIERTA THEN                                                 0000426600802112  
     BEGIN                                                              0000426700802212  
        LOCK S151BD13BIFIN:DMSTATUS;                                    0000426800802232  
        IF NOT BOOLEAN(DMSTATUS) THEN                                   0000426900802252  
        BEGIN                                                           0000427000802262  
           BEGINTRANSACTION NOAUDIT S151B99REINICTL :DMSTATUS;          0000427100802267  
           PUT S151BD13BIFIN(B00-GLO-TIEMESP := TMPO_ESPERA,            0000427200802272  
                             B00-GLO-SISTCITI:= BLOCK_ENVIOS);          0000427300802282  
           STORE S151BD13BIFIN:DMSTATUS;                                0000427400802292  
           ENDTRANSACTION AUDIT S151B99REINICTL;                        0000427500802294  
        END                                                             0000427600802297  
        ELSE                                                            0000427700802302  
           FREE S151BD13BIFIN:DMSTATUS;                                 0000427800802307  
        CLOSE S151BD13BIFIN:DMSTATUS;                                   0000427900802312  
        BD_ABIERTA := FALSE                                             0000428000802412  
     END;                                                               0000428100803012  
     CLOSE (MOVS_CDIR[0],LOCK);                                         0000428200804012  
     CLOSE (MOVS_CDIR[1],LOCK);                                         0000428300805012  
     CLOSE (SALIDA)           ;                                         0000428400805112  
     IF RESPUESTA GEQ 0 THEN                                            0000428500806012  
     BEGIN                                                              0000428600807012  
        REPLACE ACONTROL_B05 BY 48"00" FOR SIZE(ACONTROL_B05);          0000428700808012  
        REPLACE DFB05FUNCION(ACONTROL_B05) BY    22    FOR 2 DIGITS,    0000428800809012  
                                           SIST_LIB500 FOR 4 DIGITS,    0000428900810012  
                                           0           FOR 8 DIGITS,    0000429000811012  
                                           12          FOR 4 DIGITS;    0000429100812012  
        B05PROCESOS(ACONTROL_B05);                                      0000429200813012  
        RESPUESTA := DELINKLIBRARY(CONTROL);                            0000429300814012  
     END;                                                               0000429400815012  
  END OF BAJA;                                                          0000429500816012  
                                                                        0000429600817012  
  PROCEDURE ACTZA_BIFIN(CAMPO,VALOR, FEC_PROC, NUM_MSG_ENV);            0000429700818012  
  VALUE     CAMPO, VALOR, FEC_PROC, NUM_MSG_ENV;                        0000429800818022  
  INTEGER   CAMPO      , % 0,2 = ACTUALIZA ESTATUS, 1 = ACT. REENVIOS   0000429900818032  
            VALOR      , % DATO A COLOCAR EN EL CAMPO DE ESTATUS/REENVIO0000430000818042  
            FEC_PROC   , % FECHA DE PROCESO                             0000430100818052  
            NUM_MSG_ENV; % NUMERO DE MENSAGE A MODIFICA                 0000430200818062  
  BEGIN                                                                 0000430300818072  
     LOCK B04SXCTLENVIO AT                                              0000430400819012  
         (B04-CID-FECENV   = FEC_PROC     AND                           0000430500820012  
          B04-CID-CTLENVIO = NUM_MSG_ENV) :DMSTATUS;                    0000430600821012  
      IF NOT BOOLEAN(DMSTATUS) THEN                                     0000430700822012  
        BEGIN                                                           0000430800823012  
           CASE CAMPO OF                                                0000430900823022  
           BEGIN                                                        0000431000824012  
              0 : PUT S151B04CTLCITIDIR(B04-CID-ESTATUS    := VALOR);   0000431100824022  
              1 : PUT S151B04CTLCITIDIR(B04-CID-REINTENTOS := VALOR);   0000431200825022  
              2 : PUT S151B04CTLCITIDIR(B04-CID-ESTATUS    := VALOR);   0000431300825032  
           ELSE : ;                                                     0000431400825052  
           END;      % FIN CASE                                         0000431500825062  
         BEGINTRANSACTION NOAUDIT S151B99REINICTL :DMSTATUS;            0000431600831012  
         IF NOT BOOLEAN(DMSTATUS) THEN                                  0000431700831112  
         BEGIN                                                          0000431800831162  
            STORE S151B04CTLCITIDIR : DMSTATUS;                         0000431900831212  
            IF BOOLEAN(DMSTATUS) THEN                                   0000432000831262  
            BEGIN                                                       0000432100831282  
               REPLACE MSG_ERR BY STR_HDRLOG, 48"00" FOR 227;           0000432200831312  
               REPLACE MSG_ERR[23] BY "ERR UPDAT 01 CAT. "      ,       0000432300831314  
                                   DMSTATUS.DMERROR FOR 2 DIGITS,       0000432400831315  
                                   " ERROR "                    ,       0000432500831316  
                                   DMSTATUS.DMERRORTYPE                 0000432600831317  
                                   FOR 4 DIGITS                 ,       0000432700831318  
                                   " # MSG "                    ,       0000432800831319  
                                   NUM_MSG_ENV     FOR 6 DIGITS ;       0000432900831320  
               WRITE(SALIDA,40,MSG_ERR);                                0000433000831332  
               REPLACE MSG_ERR BY 48"00" FOR 250;                       0000433100831337  
            END;                                                        0000433200831342  
            ENDTRANSACTION AUDIT S151B99REINICTL;                       0000433300831362  
         END                                                            0000433400831412  
         ELSE                                                           0000433500831462  
            IF DMSTATUS.DMERROR = AUDITERROR AND                        0000433600831512  
               DMSTATUS.DMERRORTYPE = 1 THEN                            0000433700831562  
            BEGIN                                                       0000433800831612  
               STORE S151B04CTLCITIDIR : DMSTATUS;                      0000433900831662  
               IF BOOLEAN(DMSTATUS) THEN                                0000434000831712  
               BEGIN                                                    0000434100831762  
                  REPLACE MSG_ERR BY STR_HDRLOG, 48"00" FOR 227;        0000434200831772  
                  REPLACE MSG_ERR[23] BY "ERR UPDAT 02 CAT. "      ,    0000434300831773  
                                      DMSTATUS.DMERROR FOR 2 DIGITS,    0000434400831774  
                                      " ERROR "                    ,    0000434500831775  
                                      DMSTATUS.DMERRORTYPE              0000434600831776  
                                      FOR 4 DIGITS                 ,    0000434700831777  
                                      " # MSG "                    ,    0000434800831778  
                                      NUM_MSG_ENV     FOR 6 DIGITS ;    0000434900831779  
                  WRITE(SALIDA,40,MSG_ERR);                             0000435000831792  
                  REPLACE MSG_ERR BY 48"00" FOR 250;                    0000435100831797  
               END;                                                     0000435200831802  
            END;                                                        0000435300831812  
        END;                                                            0000435400831912  
      FREE S151B04CTLCITIDIR  :DMSTATUS;                                0000435500833012  
   END OF ACTZA_BIFIN;                                                  0000435600834012

  PROCEDURE ENVIA_CD(RSTL, NUM_SEC);                                    0000437200866012  
  VALUE RSTL, NUM_SEC;                                                  0000437300867012  
  INTEGER RSTL, NUM_SEC;                                                0000437400868012  
  BEGIN                                                                 0000437500869012  
                                                                        0000437600870012  
  EXCEPTION PROCEDURE LIB_EVENTO;                                       0000437700871012  
  BEGIN                                                                 0000437800871022  
     LIBERA(ACT_BIFIN);                                                 0000437900872012  
  END;                                                                  0000438000872112  
                                                                        0000438100873012  
   EBCDIC ARRAY TEXTO_RESP[0:100], MSGLJ_RESP[0:1000];                  0000438200873112  
                                                                        0000438300873212  
     PID_RESP:= GETPID(ID_RESP);                                        0000438400883032  
     RESULT_RESP_TANDEM:= RSTL;                                         0000438500884012  
     CTLENVIO_MOVTOS:= NUM_SEC;                                         0000438600885012  
     REPLACE MSGLJ_RESP BY 48"00" FOR 1000;                             0000438700885022  
     REPLACE TEXTO_RESP BY " "    FOR 100;                              0000438800885112  
     REPLACE TEXTO_RESP BY TIME(7).[23:06] FOR 2 DIGITS,                0000438900885212  
                           ":"                         ,                0000439000885312  
                           TIME(7).[17:06] FOR 2 DIGITS,                0000439100885412  
                           ":"                         ,                0000439200885422  
                           TIME(7).[11:06] FOR 2 DIGITS,                0000439300885432  
                           " MSG "                     ,                0000439400885512  
                           NUM_SEC         FOR 7 DIGITS,                0000439500885612  
                           " RESULT "                  ,                0000439600885712  
                           RSTL            FOR 4 DIGITS;                0000439700885812  
     WRITE(SALIDA,15,TEXTO_RESP);                                       0000439800885912  
     IF (CTLENVIO_MOVTOS > ULTIMO_MSG_ENV) AND                          0000439900885922  
        (MONITOR OR MONITOR_RESP)          THEN                         0000440000886012  
     BEGIN                                                              0000440100887012  
        TOT_RESP_ERR_TANDEM := * + 1;                                   0000440200888012  
        REPLACE MSGLJ_RESP  BY STR_HDRMSG                             , 0000440300889012  
                               " L012 >"                              , 0000440400890012  
                               "NUMERO DE SECUENCIA RECIBIDO ("       , 0000440500891012  
                               CTLENVIO_MOVTOS FOR 4 DIGITS           , 0000440600892012  
                               ") MAYOR A NUMERO MAXIMO DE SECUENCIA ", 0000440700893012  
                               "ENVIADO ("                            , 0000440800894012  
                               ULTIMO_MSG_ENV  FOR 6 DIGITS           , 0000440900895012  
                               ")"                                    , 0000441000896012  
                               48"00" FOR 10                          ; 0000441100896022  
        LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ_RESP);                                           0000441200897012  
     END                                                                0000441300898012  
     ELSE                                                               0000441400899012  
     BEGIN                                                              0000441500899022  
        OBTEN_EVE(PID_RESP,0);                                          0000441600899032  
        CASE RSTL OF                                                    0000441700900012  
        BEGIN                                                           0000441800901012  
          1 : BEGIN   % MOVIMIENTO RECIBIDO EN TANDEM                   0000441900902012  
                 IF MONITOR OR MONITOR_RESP THEN                        0000442000903012  
                 BEGIN                                                  0000442100904012  
                    REPLACE MSGLJ_RESP BY STR_HDRMSG                  , 0000442200905012  
                                          " L012 >"                   , 0000442300906012  
                                          "RESULT 1, SECUENCIA # "    , 0000442400907012  
                                          CTLENVIO_MOVTOS FOR 4 DIGITS, 0000442500908012  
                                          " ACEPTADA EN TANDEM"       , 0000442600909012  
                                          48"00" FOR 2                ; 0000442700909022  
                    LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ_RESP);                               0000442800910012  
                    REPLACE TEXTO_RESP BY " " FOR 100;                  0000442900910112  
                    REPLACE TEXTO_RESP BY TIME(7).[23:06] FOR 2 DIGITS, 0000443000910212  
                                          ":",                          0000443100910232  
                                          TIME(7).[17:06] FOR 2 DIGITS, 0000443200910252  
                                          ":",                          0000443300910262  
                                          TIME(7).[11:06] FOR 2 DIGITS, 0000443400910266  
                                          " ",                          0000443500910268  
                                          MSGLJ_RESP[23] UNTIL= 48"00"; 0000443600910272  
                    WRITE(SALIDA,15,TEXTO_RESP);                        0000443700910312  
                 END;                                                   0000443800911012  
                 ACTZA_BIFIN(0,1,FECHA_PROCESO,CTLENVIO_MOVTOS);        0000443900914012  
                 TOT_TANDEM        := * + 1;                            0000444000916012  
              END;                                                      0000444100917012  
          2 : BEGIN   %MOVIMIENTO RECHAZADO EN TANDEM, SE ACTIVA REENVIO0000444200918012  
                 RECHAZADO := TRUE;                                     0000444300918022  
                 IF MONITOR OR MONITOR_RESP THEN                        0000444400919012  
                 BEGIN                                                  0000444500920012  
                    REPLACE MSGLJ_RESP BY STR_HDRMSG,                   0000444600921012  
                                          "L012>RESULT 2 SECUENCIA # ", 0000444700922012  
                                          CTLENVIO_MOVTOS FOR 4 DIGITS, 0000444800923012  
                                          " RECHAZADA EN TANDEM"      , 0000444900924012  
                                          48"00" FOR 2                ; 0000445000925012  
                    LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ_RESP);                               0000445100926012  
                    REPLACE TEXTO_RESP BY " " FOR 100;                  0000445200926062  
                    REPLACE TEXTO_RESP BY TIME(7).[23:06] FOR 2 DIGITS, 0000445300926112  
                                          ":",                          0000445400926162  
                                          TIME(7).[17:06] FOR 2 DIGITS, 0000445500926212  
                                          ":",                          0000445600926232  
                                          TIME(7).[11:06] FOR 2 DIGITS, 0000445700926242  
                                          " ",                          0000445800926252  
                                          MSGLJ_RESP[23] UNTIL= 48"00"; 0000445900926262  
                    WRITE(SALIDA,15,TEXTO_RESP);                        0000446000926312  
                 END;                                                   0000446100927012  
                 ACTZA_BIFIN(0,2,FECHA_PROCESO,CTLENVIO_MOVTOS);        0000446200930012  
%               TOT_TANDEM        := * + 1;                             0000446300932012  
                TOT_ERRORES := * + 1;                                   0000446400933012  
              END;                                                      0000446500934012  
       3 : 4: BEGIN   % MOVIMIENTO DUPLICADO                            0000446600935012  
                 IF MONITOR OR MONITOR_RESP THEN                        0000446700936012  
                 BEGIN                                                  0000446800937012  
                    REPLACE MSGLJ_RESP BY STR_HDRMSG,                   0000446900938012  
                                      " L012 >RESULT 3, SECUENCIA # ",  0000447000939012  
                                      CTLENVIO_MOVTOS FOR 4 DIGITS   ,  0000447100940012  
                                      " DUPLICADA EN TANDEM"         ,  0000447200941012  
                                      48"00" FOR 10                  ;  0000447300942012  
                    LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ_RESP);                               0000447400943012  
                    REPLACE TEXTO_RESP BY " " FOR 100;                  0000447500943022  
                    REPLACE TEXTO_RESP BY TIME(7).[23:06] FOR 2 DIGITS, 0000447600943032  
                                          ":",                          0000447700943042  
                                          TIME(7).[17:06] FOR 2 DIGITS, 0000447800943052  
                                          ":",                          0000447900943057  
                                          TIME(7).[11:06] FOR 2 DIGITS, 0000448000943058  
                                          " ",                          0000448100943060  
                                          MSGLJ[23] UNTIL = 48"00";     0000448200943062  
                    WRITE(SALIDA,15,TEXTO_RESP);                        0000448300943112  
                 END;                                                   0000448400944012  
                 ACTZA_BIFIN(0,3,FECHA_PROCESO,CTLENVIO_MOVTOS);        0000448500947012  
%               TOT_TANDEM     := * + 1;                                0000448600949012  
                TOT_DUPLICADOS := * + 1;                                0000448700950012  
              END;                                                      0000448800951012  
                                                                        0000448900952012  
         ELSE :BEGIN                                                    0000449000953012  
                 TOT_RESP_ERR_TANDEM := * + 1;                          0000449100954012  
                 IF MONITOR OR MONITOR_RESP THEN                        0000449200955012  
                 BEGIN                                                  0000449300956012  
                    REPLACE MSGLJ_RESP BY STR_HDRMSG                   ,0000449400957012  
                                          " L012 >"                    ,0000449500958012  
                                          "RESULT. NO CONSIDERADO EN " ,0000449600959012  
                                          "RESP. DE TANDEM RESULT = "  ,0000449700960012  
                                          RSTL FOR 4 DIGITS            ,0000449800961012  
                                          " EN SECUENCIA "             ,0000449900962012  
                                          NUM_MSG FOR 4 DIGITS         ,0000450000963012  
                                          48"00" FOR 10                ;0000450100963022  
                    LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ_RESP);                               0000450200964012  
                    REPLACE TEXTO_RESP BY " " FOR 100;                  0000450300964112  
                    REPLACE TEXTO_RESP BY TIME(7).[23:06] FOR 2 DIGITS, 0000450400964212  
                                          ":",                          0000450500964312  
                                          TIME(7).[17:06] FOR 2 DIGITS, 0000450600964412  
                                          ":",                          0000450700964462  
                                          TIME(7).[11:06] FOR 2 DIGITS, 0000450800964472  
                                          " ",                          0000450900964482  
                                          MSGLJ[23] UNTIL = 48"00";     0000451000964512  
                    WRITE(SALIDA,15,TEXTO_RESP);                        0000451100964612  
                 END;                                                   0000451200965012  
               END;                                                     0000451300966012  
        END;  %FIN DEL CASE                                             0000451400967012  
        LIBERA(ACT_BIFIN);                                              0000451500967022  
     END;     %FIN DEL ELSE                                             0000451600968012  
  END OF ENVIA_CD;                                                      0000451700969012
