  PROCEDURE VERIFICA_B05;                                               0000451900971012  
  BEGIN                                                                 0000452000972012  
     DEFINE SNR = MYSELF.MIXNUMBER#;                                    0000452100973012  
                                                                        0000452200974012  
     ARRAY   ARR1,ARR2[0:200],           % ARREGLO PARA EJEC. DE DCKEY  0000452300975012  
             TOKEN    [0:79],            % PALABRA DE RESPUESTA         0000452400976012  
             NOM_LIB,                                                   0000452500977012  
             MIX_SIN_MTP,                                               0000452600978012  
             LIB_SIN_MTP[0:71];          % NOMBRE DE LA LIBRERIA        0000452700979012  
                                                                        0000452800980012  
     STRING  STR_LIB,                                                   0000452900981012  
             STR_MIX;                                                   0000453000982012  
                                                                        0000453100983012  
     INTEGER RESULT_CTL,                 % RESULTADO DE LLAMADO A L001  0000453200984012  
             MIX_L012;                   % MEZCLA DE LIBRERIA           0000453300985012  
                                                                        0000453400986012  
                                                                        0000453500987012  
     POINTER P_SAL;                      % APUNTADOR A RESP. DE DCKEY   0000453600988012  
                                                                        0000453700989012  
     REPLACE NOM_LIB BY MYSELF.NAME;                                    0000453800990012  
     REPLACE LIB_SIN_MTP BY POINTER(NOM_LIB) FOR 33;                    0000453900991012  
     REPLACE ACONTROL_B05 BY 48"00" FOR SIZE(ACONTROL_B05);             0000454000992012  
     REPLACE DFB05FUNCION(ACONTROL_B05)                                 0000454100993012  
             BY     1 FOR 2 DIGITS,                                     0000454200994012  
                    SIST_LIB500 FOR 4 DIGITS,                           0000454300995012  
                    0           FOR 8 DIGITS,                           0000454400996012  
                    12          FOR 4 DIGITS;                           0000454500997012  
     RESULT_CTL:= B05PROCESOS(ACONTROL_B05);                            0000454600998012  
     WHILE RESULT_CTL = 0 DO                                            0000454700999012  
     BEGIN                                                              0000454801000012  
        MIX_L012 := IDFB05MIX(ACONTROL_B05);                            0000454901001012  
        REPLACE ARR1 BY " " FOR 201 WORDS  ;                            0000455001002012  
        REPLACE ARR1 BY MIX_L012   FOR 5 DIGITS,                        0000455101003012  
                    "Y"                        ,                        0000455201004012  
                    48"00"                     ;                        0000455301005012  
        DCKEYIN(POINTER(ARR1),ARR2);                                    0000455401006012  
        P_SAL := POINTER(ARR2);                                         0000455501007012  
        REPLACE TOKEN BY 48"00" FOR 80;                                 0000455601008012  
        REPLACE TOKEN BY P_SAL:P_SAL UNTIL = " ";                       0000455701009012  
        IF TOKEN = "INVALID" OR TOKEN = "Task" THEN                     0000455801010012  
        BEGIN                                                           0000455901011012  
           REPLACE ACONTROL_B05 BY 48"00" FOR SIZE(ACONTROL_B05);       0000456001012012  
           REPLACE DFB05FUNCION(ACONTROL_B05)                           0000456101013012  
                   BY    22 FOR 2 DIGITS,                               0000456201014012  
                         SIST_LIB500 FOR 4 DIGITS,                      0000456301015012  
                         0           FOR 8 DIGITS,                      0000456401016012  
                         12          FOR 4 DIGITS;                      0000456501017012  
           RESULT_CTL := B05PROCESOS(ACONTROL_B05);                     0000456601018012  
        END                                                             0000456701019012  
        ELSE                                                            0000456801020012  
        BEGIN                                                           0000456901021012  
           REPLACE TOKEN BY 48"00" FOR 80;                              0000457001022012  
           REPLACE TOKEN BY P_SAL:P_SAL WHILE > 48"00";                 0000457101023012  
           REPLACE TOKEN BY 48"00" FOR 80;                              0000457201024012  
           REPLACE TOKEN BY P_SAL:P_SAL UNTIL = " ";                    0000457301025012  
           P_SAL := P_SAL + 1;                                          0000457401026012  
           REPLACE TOKEN BY 48"00" FOR 80;                              0000457501027012  
           REPLACE TOKEN BY P_SAL:P_SAL UNTIL = " ";                    0000457601028012  
           P_SAL := P_SAL + 1;                                          0000457701029012  
           REPLACE TOKEN BY 48"00" FOR 80;                              0000457801030012  
           REPLACE MIX_SIN_MTP BY P_SAL:P_SAL FOR 33;                   0000457901031012  
           STR_LIB := STRING(LIB_SIN_MTP,35);                           0000458001032012  
           STR_MIX := STRING(MIX_SIN_MTP,35);                           0000458101033012  
           IF STR_MIX = STR_LIB THEN                                    0000458201034012  
           BEGIN                                                        0000458301035012  
              REPLACE ARR1 BY " " FOR 201 WORDS;                        0000458401036012  
              REPLACE ARR1 BY MIX_L012   FOR 5 DIGITS,                  0000458501037012  
                             "HI 4"                 ,                   0000458601038012  
                             48"00";                                    0000458701039012  
              DCKEYIN(POINTER(ARR1),ARR2);                              0000458801040012  
              WAIT((3));                                                0000458901041012  
              DCKEYIN(POINTER(ARR1),ARR2);                              0000459001042012  
              IF  ARR2 = "INVALID NUMBER" THEN                          0000459101043012  
              BEGIN                                                     0000459201044012  
                 REPLACE ACONTROL_B05 BY 48"00"                         0000459301045012  
                                       FOR SIZE(ACONTROL_B05);          0000459401046012  
                  REPLACE DFB05FUNCION(ACONTROL_B05)                    0000459501047012  
                          BY    22 FOR 2 DIGITS,                        0000459601048012  
                                SIST_LIB500 FOR 4 DIGITS,               0000459701049012  
                                0           FOR 8 DIGITS,               0000459801050012  
                                12          FOR 4 DIGITS;               0000459901051012  
                  RESULT_CTL := B05PROCESOS(ACONTROL_B05);              0000460001052012  
              END                                                       0000460101053012  
              ELSE                                                      0000460201054012  
              BEGIN                                                     0000460301055012  
                 REPLACE MSGLJ BY STR_HDRMSG, 48"00" FOR 227;           0000460401056012  
                 REPLACE TXTMSG BY " L012 > ",                          0000460501057012  
                                  "ERROR L012/S",                       0000460601058012  
                                  SIST_LIB FOR 3 DIGITS,                0000460701059012  
                                  ", EXISTE UNA L012 CON MIX ",         0000460801060012  
                                  MIX_L012   FOR 4 DIGITS,              0000460901061012  
                                  " DARLA DE BAJA PARA PODER CONT. ";   0000461001062012  
                 LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                       0000461101063012  
                 ACCEPT(TXTMSG);                                        0000461201064012  
              END;                                                      0000461301065012  
              REPLACE ACONTROL_B05 BY 48"00" FOR SIZE(ACONTROL_B05);    0000461401066012  
              REPLACE DFB05FUNCION(ACONTROL_B05)                        0000461501067012  
                      BY     1 FOR 2 DIGITS,                            0000461601068012  
                             SIST_LIB500 FOR 4 DIGITS,                  0000461701069012  
                             0           FOR 8 DIGITS,                  0000461801070012  
                             12          FOR 4 DIGITS;                  0000461901071012  
              RESULT_CTL:= B05PROCESOS(ACONTROL_B05);                   0000462001072012  
           END                                                          0000462101073012  
           ELSE                                                         0000462201074012  
           BEGIN                                                        0000462301075012  
              REPLACE ACONTROL_B05 BY 48"00" FOR SIZE(ACONTROL_B05);    0000462401076012  
              REPLACE DFB05FUNCION(ACONTROL_B05)                        0000462501077012  
                      BY    22 FOR 2 DIGITS,                            0000462601078012  
                            SIST_LIB500 FOR 4 DIGITS,                   0000462701079012  
                            0           FOR 8 DIGITS,                   0000462801080012  
                            12          FOR 4 DIGITS;                   0000462901081012  
              RESULT_CTL:= B05PROCESOS(ACONTROL_B05);                   0000463001082012  
           END;                                                         0000463101083012  
        END;                                                            0000463201084012  
     END;                                                               0000463301085012  
     REPLACE ACONTROL_B05 BY 48"00" FOR SIZE(ACONTROL_B05);             0000463401086012  
     REPLACE DFB05FUNCION(ACONTROL_B05)                                 0000463501087012  
             BY    19          FOR 2 DIGITS,                            0000463601088012  
                   SIST_LIB500 FOR 4 DIGITS,                            0000463701089012  
                   0           FOR 8 DIGITS,                            0000463801090112  
                   12          FOR 4 DIGITS,                            0000463901090212  
                   "    ",                                              0000464001090312  
                   0           FOR 2 DIGITS,                            0000464101090412  
                   SNR         FOR 8 DIGITS,                            0000464201090512

                   20000       FOR 6 DIGITS,                            0000464401090712  
                   0           FOR 6 DIGITS,                            0000464501090812  
                   220000      FOR 6 DIGITS,                            0000464601090912  
                   0           FOR 8 DIGITS,                            0000464701091012  
                   0           FOR 8 DIGITS;                            0000464801092012  
     RESULT_CTL:= B05PROCESOS(ACONTROL_B05);                            0000464901095012  
     IF RESULT_CTL > 0 THEN                                             0000465001096012  
     BEGIN                                                              0000465101097012

          REPLACE TXTMSG BY " L012 > ERROR EN CTLVER," ,                0000470001146012  
                           " NO EXISTE IDENTIFICADOR DE " ,             0000470101147012  
                           "CTLVER_NOM CONTROL S151 (S151L001CTL)";     0000470201148012  
          LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                              0000470301149012  
          REPLACE CONTROL.TITLE BY "(S151)S151/OBJECT/L001/CONTROL.";   0000470401150012  
       END                                                              0000470501151012  
       ELSE                                                             0000470601152012  
          REPLACE CONTROL.TITLE BY CTLVER_NOM FOR 72;                   0000470701153012  
                                                                        0000470801154012  
    END OF VERSIONES;                                                   0000470901155012  
                                                                        0000471001156012  
                                                                        0000471101157012  
                                                                        0000471201158012  
%====================================================================   0000471301159012  
%      PROCESO DE INICIO DE LIBRERIA                                =   0000471401160012  
%====================================================================   0000471501161012  
 PROCEDURE INICIA;                                                      0000471601162012  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000471701163012  
    EBCDIC ARRAY   DATOS_HOST[0:42], PK_MOV[0:17];                      0000471801164012  
    INTEGER        AA, MM, DD, INT,                                     0000471901165012  
                   RESULT_CTL;                                          0000472001166012  
                                                                        0000472101167012  
    PROCEDURE CONSULTA_B01;                                             0000472201168012  
    BEGIN                                                               0000472301169012  
       EBCDIC ARRAY ACONTROL[0:560];                                    0000472401170012  
       REPLACE SISDFUNCI(ACONTROL) BY       1 FOR 2 DIGITS,             0000472501171012  
                                     SIST_LIB FOR 4 DIGITS,             0000472601172012  
                                     CSI_LIB  FOR 2 DIGITS;             0000472701173012  
       RESULT_CTL:= CONSISDIA(ACONTROL);                                0000472801174012  
       IF RESULT_CTL > 0 THEN                                           0000472901175012  
       BEGIN                                                            0000473001176012  
          REPLACE MSGLJ BY STR_HDRMSG, 48"00" FOR 227;                  0000473101177012  
          REPLACE TXTMSG BY " L012 > ERROR EN CONSULTA ",               0000473201178012  
                           "DE LIB S151/L001, SISTEMA S",               0000473301179012  
                           SIST_LIB FOR 3 DIGITS,                       0000473401180012  
                           RESULT_CTL FOR 6 DIGITS;                     0000473501181012  
          LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                              0000473601182012  
          MYSELF.STATUS:= -1;                                           0000473701183012  
       END;                                                             0000473801184012  
       FECHA_PROCESO := ISISDFECCON(ACONTROL);                          0000473901185012  
       REPLACE PK_MOV  BY SISDPKMOVS(ACONTROL) FOR 17;                  0000474001186012  
                                                                        0000474101187012  
    END OF CONSULTA_B01;                                                0000474201188012  
                                                                        0000474301189012  
  PROCEDURE CONSULTA_HORAS;                                             0000474401190012  
  BEGIN                                                                 0000474501191012  
     REPLACE ACONTROL_B05 BY 48"00" FOR SIZE(ACONTROL_B05);             0000474601192012  
     REPLACE DFB05FUNCION(ACONTROL_B05)                                 0000474701193012  
             BY     1 FOR 2 DIGITS,                                     0000474801194012  
                    SIST_LIB500 FOR 4 DIGITS,                           0000474901195012  
                    0           FOR 8 DIGITS,                           0000475001196012  
                    0           FOR 4 DIGITS;                           0000475101197012  
     RESULT_CTL:= B05PROCESOS(ACONTROL_B05);                            0000475201198012  
     IF RESULT_CTL = 0 THEN                                             0000475301199012  
     BEGIN                                                              0000475401200012  
        HORA_INICIO :=INTEGERT(IDFB05HORAINI(ACONTROL_B05)/100);        0000475501201012  
        HORA_FIN    :=INTEGERT(IDFB05HORAFIN(ACONTROL_B05)/100);        0000475601202012  
     END                                                                0000475701203012  
     ELSE                                                               0000475801204012  
     BEGIN                                                              0000475901205012  
        REPLACE ACONTROL_B05 BY 48"00" FOR SIZE(ACONTROL_B05);          0000476001206012  
        REPLACE DFB05FUNCION(ACONTROL_B05)                              0000476101207012  
                BY    19          FOR 2 DIGITS,                         0000476201208012  
                      SIST_LIB500 FOR 4 DIGITS,                         0000476301209012  
                      0           FOR 8 DIGITS,                         0000476401210012  
                      12          FOR 4 DIGITS,                         0000476501212012  
                      "    "                  ,                         0000476601213012  
                      0           FOR 2 DIGITS,                         0000476701213022  
                      SNR         FOR 8 DIGITS,                         0000476801214012  
                      0           FOR 6 DIGITS,                         0000476901215012  
                      20000       FOR 6 DIGITS,                         0000477001216012  
                      0           FOR 6 DIGITS,                         0000477101217012  
                      220000      FOR 6 DIGITS,                         0000477201218012  
                      0           FOR 8 DIGITS,                         0000477301219012  
                      0           FOR 8 DIGITS;                         0000477401220012  
        RESULT_CTL:= B05PROCESOS(ACONTROL_B05);                         0000477501221012  
        IF RESULT_CTL > 0 THEN                                          0000477601222012  
        BEGIN                                                           0000477701223012  
           REPLACE MSGLJ BY STR_HDRMSG, 48"00" FOR 227;                 0000477801224012  
           REPLACE TXTMSG BY " L012 > ",                                0000477901225012  
                             "NO QUEDO REGISTRADO EL HORARIO DEFAULT",  0000478001226012  
                             " DE TRANFERENCIA EN BASE DE CONTROL ";    0000478101227012  
           LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                             0000478201228012  
           MYSELF.STATUS:= -1;                                          0000478301229012  
        END;                                                            0000478401230012  
        HORA_INICIO := 200;                                             0000478501231012  
        HORA_FIN    := 2200;                                            0000478601232012  
     END;                                                               0000478701233012  
  END OF CONSULTA_HORAS;                                                0000478801234012  
%=================================================================      0000478901235012  
%   PROCEDURE ASIGNA ARCHIVOS                                    =      0000479001236012  
%=================================================================      0000479101237012  
    PROCEDURE ASIGNA_ARCHIVOS;                                          0000479201238012  
    BEGIN                                                               0000479301239012  
       IF A = 0 THEN                                                    0000479401240012  
          SIST_LIB := SIST_LIB264                                       0000479501241012  
       ELSE                                                             0000479601242012  
          SIST_LIB := SIST_LIB500;                                      0000479701243012  
                                                                        0000479801243312  
       REPLACE NOMB_ARC_CDIR BY " " FOR 72;                             0000479901244012  
       REPLACE NOMB_ARC_CDIR BY "(S151)S151/FILE/CTDS",                 0000480001245012  
                             SIST_LIB FOR 3 DIGITS,                     0000480101246012  
                             "/",                                       0000480201247012  
                             FECHA_PROCESO  FOR 6 DIGITS,               0000480301248012  
                             " ON "              ,                      0000480401249012  
                             PK_MOV  FOR 17,"."  ;                      0000480501250012  
       IF MOVS_CDIR[A].OPEN THEN                                        0000480601250022  
          CLOSE(MOVS_CDIR[A]);  %SE CIERRE Y SE LIBERA ARCHIVO          0000480701250032  
       REPLACE MOVS_CDIR[A].TITLE BY NOMB_ARC_CDIR;                     0000480801251012  
                                                                        0000480901251112  
       REPLACE NOMB_ARC_MONITOR BY "(S151)S151/FILE/MONITOR/L012/",     0000481001251212  
                                   FECHA_PROCESO FOR 6 DIGITS,          0000481101251312  
                                   " ON CMEMP.";                        0000481201251412  
       IF SALIDA.OPEN THEN                                              0000481301251422  
          CLOSE(SALIDA,LOCK);    %SE CIERRA Y SE LIBERA ARCHIVO         0000481401251432  
       REPLACE SALIDA.TITLE BY NOMB_ARC_MONITOR;                        0000481501251512  
       IF NOT SALIDA.RESIDENT THEN                                      0000481601251532  
       BEGIN                                                            0000481701251552  
          SALIDA.MYUSE := 2 ;                                           0000481801251562  
          OPEN(SALIDA,WAIT) ;                                           0000481901251612  
          CLOSE(SALIDA)     ;                                           0000482001251662  
          SALIDA.MYUSE := 3 ;                                           0000482101251672  
       END                                                              0000482201251682  
       ELSE                                                             0000482301251692  
       IF NOT SALIDA.OPEN THEN                                          0000482401251722  
       BEGIN                                                            0000482501251728  
          OPEN(SALIDA,WAIT) ;                                           0000482601251730  
          LAST_RECORD := SALIDA.LASTRECORD;                             0000482701251732  
          IF LAST_RECORD > 0  THEN                                      0000482801251752  
             SEEK(SALIDA[LAST_RECORD]);                                 0000482901251762  
       END;                                                             0000483001251862  
    END OF ASIGNA_ARCHIVOS;                                             0000483101252012  
                                                                        0000483201253012  
                                                                        0000483301254012  
    PROCEDURE VALIDA_ARCHIVOS;                                          0000483401255012  
    BEGIN                                                               0000483501256012  
      BOOLEAN CICLO;                                                    0000483601258012  
      A:= 1;                                                            0000483701258112  
      AUTS151_ENVIOS := 0;                                              0000483801260012  
      NUMERO_ARCHIVO := 0;                                              0000483901261012  
      NREG_FISICO := 0;                                                 0000484001261112  
      NMSG_DESGLOSADO:= 0;                                              0000484101261212  
      NREG_MOVS[A] := 1;                                                0000484201261262  
      LOCALIZA_FIRST_BIFIN;                                             0000484301262012  
      WHILE NOT BOOLEAN(DMSTATUS) DO                                    0000484401263012  
      BEGIN                                                             0000484501264012  
        GET S151B04CTLCITIDIR( IDFAUTS151 := B04-CID-CTLFILE,           0000484601265012  
                           AUTS151_ENVIOS := B04-CID-CTLFILE,           0000484701265062  
                              NREG_FISICO := B04-CID-CTLFILE,           0000484801265112  
                               NUMCTENVIO := B04-CID-CTLENVIO,          0000484901266012  
                          NMSG_DESGLOSADO := B04-CID-CTLENVIO,          0000485001266112  
                               IDFESTATUS := B04-CID-ESTATUS);          0000485101267012  
        CASE IDFESTATUS OF                                              0000485201273012  
        BEGIN                                                           0000485301274012  
               1 : TOT_TANDEM := TOT_LEIDOS := TOT_ENVIADOS := * + 1;   0000485401276012  
               2 : TOT_LEIDOS := TOT_ENVIADOS := TOT_ERRORES := * + 1;  0000485501282012  
               3 : TOT_DUPLICADOS := * + 1;                             0000485601284012  
            ELSE : ;                                                    0000485701287012  
        END;                                                            0000485801288012  
        LOCALIZA_NEXT_BIFIN; %EN VALIDA_ARCHIVO                         0000485901289012  
      END;  %DEL WHILE                                                  0000486001290012  
      ULTIMO_MSG_ENV := NMSG_DESGLOSADO;                                0000486101290022  
     FOR A:= 1 STEP 1 UNTIL 1 DO                                        0000486201291012  
     BEGIN                                                              0000486301292012  
      CICLO := TRUE;                                                    0000486401293012  
      WHILE CICLO DO                                                    0000486501294012  
      BEGIN                                                             0000486601295012  
         IF MOVS_CDIR[A].RESIDENT THEN                                  0000486701296012  
         BEGIN                                                          0000486801297012  
            NREG_MOVS[A] := 0;                                          0000486901298012  
            CLOSE(MOVS_CDIR[A],LOCK);                                   0000487001299012  
            MOVS_CDIR[A].BLOCKSIZE := 210;                              0000487101300012  
%            READ(MOVS_CDIR[A][NREG_MOVS[A]],165,ALOG_CDIR);            0000487201301012  
            READ(MOVS_CDIR[A][NREG_MOVS[A]],210,ALOG_CDIR);             0000487301301512  
            IF IDFFUNCION(ALOG_CDIR) NEQ 99 THEN                        0000487401302012  
            BEGIN                                                       0000487501303012  
               WHILE HDFECLOG(ALOG_CDIR) NEQ FECHA_PROCESO DO           0000487601304012  
               BEGIN                                                    0000487701305012  
                  REPLACE MSGLJ BY STR_HDRMSG, 48"00" FOR 227;          0000487801306012  
                  REPLACE TXTMSG BY " L012 > ",                         0000487901307012  
                                   "ERROR EN HEADER ARCHIVO ",          0000488001308012  
                                   MOVS_CDIR[A].TITLE,                  0000488101309012  
                                   ", FECHA INTERNA ",                  0000488201310012  
                                   HDFECLOG(ALOG_CDIR) FOR 8 DIGITS,    0000488301311012  
                                   " FECHA CORRECTA ",                  0000488401312012  
                                   FECHA_PROCESO FOR 8 DIGITS;          0000488501313012  
                  LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                      0000488601314012  
                  CLOSE(MOVS_CDIR[A],LOCK);                             0000488701315012  
                  ACCEPT(TXTMSG);                                       0000488801316012  
%                  READ(MOVS_CDIR[A][NREG_MOVS[A]],165,ALOG_CDIR);      0000488901317012  
                  READ(MOVS_CDIR[A][NREG_MOVS[A]],210,ALOG_CDIR);       0000489001317512  
               END;                                                     0000489101318012  
            END;                                                        0000489201319012  
            CICLO := FALSE;                                             0000489301320012  
         END                                                            0000489401321012  
         ELSE                                                           0000489501322012  
         BEGIN                                                          0000489601323012  
            REPLACE MSGLJ  BY STR_HDRMSG, 48"00" FOR 227;               0000489701324012  
            REPLACE TXTMSG BY " L012 > ",                               0000489801325012  
                              "ERROR NO EXISTE ARCHIVO ",               0000489901326012  
                              MOVS_CDIR[A].TITLE,                       0000490001327012  
                              " FAVOR DE PONERLO RESIDENTE";            0000490101328012  
             LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                           0000490201329012  
             ACCEPT(MSGLJ);                                             0000490301330012  
             REPLACE MOVS_CDIR[A].TITLE   BY NOMB_ARC_CDIR;             0000490401331012  
         END;                                                           0000490501332012  
      END;                                                              0000490601333012  
      NREG_MOVS[A] := 1;                                                0000490701342012  
      CLOSE(MOVS_CDIR[A],LOCK);                                         0000490801342112  
      MOVS_CDIR[A].BLOCKSIZE := 210;                                    0000490901342212  
%      READ(MOVS_CDIR[A][NREG_MOVS[A]],165,ALOG_CDIR);                  0000491001342312  
      READ(MOVS_CDIR[A][NREG_MOVS[A]],210,ALOG_CDIR);                   0000491101342362  
      WHILE  (AUTS151_ENVIOS > IDFAUT(ALOG_CDIR)) AND                   0000491201342412  
             IDFFUNCION(ALOG_CDIR) NEQ 99        DO                     0000491301344012  
      BEGIN                                                             0000491401345012  
        IF IDFFUNCION(ALOG_CDIR) = 50 THEN                              0000491501346012  
           SBC_LIBERADO := TRUE;                                        0000491601347012  
%        READ(MOVS_CDIR[A][NREG_MOVS[A]],165,ALOG_CDIR);                0000491701348012  
        READ(MOVS_CDIR[A][NREG_MOVS[A]],210,ALOG_CDIR);                 0000491801348512  
        NREG_MOVS[A] := * + 1;                                          0000491901349012  
      END;                                                              0000492001350012  
     END; % Del FOR A:= 0 ...                                           0000492101357012  
     A := 1;                                                            0000492201357112  
    END OF VALIDA_ARCHIVOS;                                             0000492301358012  
                                                                        0000492401359012  
%=================================================================      0000492501360012  
% INICIA CODIGO DE PROCEDIMIENTO INICIA                          =      0000492601361012  
%=================================================================      0000492701362012  
    WHILE NOT BD_ABIERTA DO                                             0000492801362112  
    BEGIN                                                               0000492901362212  
       OPEN UPDATE S151BD13BIFIN:DMSTATUS;                              0000493001363012  
       IF BOOLEAN(DMSTATUS) THEN                                        0000493101363062  
          WAIT((1))                                                     0000493201363112  
       ELSE                                                             0000493301363212  
          BD_ABIERTA := TRUE;                                           0000493401363312  
    END;                                                                0000493501363412  
    FIND S151BD13BIFIN : DMSTATUS;                                      0000493601363462  
    IF BOOLEAN(DMSTATUS) THEN                                           0000493701363512  
       TMPO_ESPERA := 1                                                 0000493801363562  
    ELSE                                                                0000493901363612  
    BEGIN                                                               0000494001363632  
       GET S151BD13BIFIN( TMPO_ESPERA := B00-GLO-TIEMESP,               0000494101363662  
                          BLOCK_ENVIOS:= B00-GLO-SISTCITI);             0000494201363682  
                                                                        0000494301363702  
    TMPO_ESPERA  := 1  ;                                                0000494401363862  
%   BLOCK_ENVIOS := 500;                                                0000494501363902  
    TRANS_X_SEG  := BLOCK_ENVIOS / TMPO_ESPERA;                         0000494601363922  
                                                                        0000494701363942  
    END;                                                                0000494801363962  
    RESET(LIBERA_SBC);                                                  0000494901364012  
    SBC_LIBERADO := MONITOR := MONITOR_RESP := MONITOR_ENVIOS :=        0000495001366012  
    LIB_SBC      := REENVIA_MOVTOS := FALSE;                            0000495101367012  
    TOT_ENVIADOS := TOT_ERRORES    := TOT_REENVIOS := REENVIOS :=       0000495201368012  
    NUMERO_ARCHIVO := NREG_MOVS[0] := TOT_ERRORES_TCP := TOT_TANDEM   :=0000495301369012  
    TOT_DUPLICADOS := TOT_RESP_ERR_TANDEM := NREG_MOVS[1] :=            0000495401370012  
    FUNCION50 := NREG_FISICO  := TOT_LEIDOS := TOT_ENVIOS := 0;         0000495501370112  
    VERSIONES;                                                          0000495601371012  
    AA := TIME(7).[47:12];                                              0000495701372012  
    MM := TIME(7).[35:06];                                              0000495801373012  
    DD := TIME(7).[29:06];                                              0000495901374012  
    REPLACE AFECHA_MAQUINA BY AA FOR 4 DIGITS,                          0000496001375012  
                              MM FOR 2 DIGITS,                          0000496101376012  
                              DD FOR 2 DIGITS;                          0000496201377012  
    FECHA_MAQ_8D := INTEGER(POINTER(AFECHA_MAQUINA),8);                 0000496301378012  
    FECHA_MAQ_6D := INTEGER(POINTER(AFECHA_MAQUINA) + 2,6);             0000496401379012  
                                                                        0000496501380012  
    INT := LINKLIBRARY(SOPORTE);                                        0000496601381012  
    CSIID(DATOS_HOST);                                                  0000496701382012  
    INT := DELINKLIBRARY(SOPORTE);                                      0000496801383012  
    CSI_LIB := INTEGER(DATOS_HOST,2);                                   0000496901384012  
    REPLACE EQUIPO BY DATOS_HOST[2] FOR 10;                             0000497001384112  
    STR_EQUIPO := STRING(EQUIPO,10);                                    0000497101384122  
    IF STR_EQUIPO = "ACYPGAMA. " OR                                     0000497201384212  
       STR_EQUIPO = "MONALFA.  " THEN                                   0000497301384312  
       CSI_DEST  := 21                                                  0000497401384412  
    ELSE                                                                0000497501384512  
       CSI_DEST  := 22;                                                 0000497601384612  
                                                                        0000497701384712  
    INT := LINKLIBRARY(CONTROL);                                        0000497801385012  
    CONSULTA_B01;                                                       0000497901386012  
%   CONSULTA_HORAS;                                                     0000498001387012  
    INT := DELINKLIBRARY(CONTROL);                                      0000498101388012  
    INT := LINKLIBRARY(S080L710);                                       0000498201390012  
    CARGA_CLAVES;                                                       0000498301391012  
    CLAVES_BNX_CITY;                                                    0000498401392012  
    INT := DELINKLIBRARY(S080L710);                                     0000498501393012  
    FIND FIRST B04SXSISTFEC AT  B04-CID-SISTEMA = SIST_LIB              0000498601395012  
                            AND B04-CID-FECENV  = FECHA_PROCESO         0000498701396012  
                            :   DMSTATUS;                               0000498801397012  
%   FOR A:= 0 STEP 1 UNTIL 1 DO                                         0000498901398012  
    A:= 1;                                                              0000499001399012  
        ASIGNA_ARCHIVOS;                                                0000499101400012  
    VALIDA_ARCHIVOS;                                                    0000499201401012  
    REINTENTOS   := 0;                                                  0000499301405012  
    IF NREG_FISICO = 0 THEN     %EN INICIA                              0000499401405112  
       NREG_MOVS[1] := 1                                                0000499501405212  
    ELSE                                                                0000499601405312  
       NREG_MOVS[1] := NREG_FISICO + 1;   %EN INICIA                    0000499701405412  
 END OF INICIA;                                                         0000499801406012  
%=====================================================================  0000499901407012  
%     PROCEDIMIENTO CARGA CLAVES BANAMEX                             =  0000500001408012  
%=====================================================================  0000500101409012  
 PROCEDURE CARGA_CLAVES;                                                0000500201410012  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000500301411012  
  INTEGER        SIST,          % SISTEMA                               0000500401412012  
                 PROG,          % PROGRAMA LLAMADOR                     0000500501413012  
                 RESULTADO,     % RESULTADO LLAMADA                     0000500601414012  
                 NIVINI,        % NIVEL INICIAL                         0000500701415012  
                 CLAVETR,       % CLAVE DE TRANSACCION                  0000500801416012  
                 I,             % INDICE                                0000500901417012  
                 FORMATO,       % FORMATO DE CATALOGO                   0000501001418012  
                 NUMCAT,        % NUMERO DE CATALOGO                    0000501101419012  
                 FECINI,        % FECHA INICIO                          0000501201420012  
                 IND_EDOCTA,    % INDICADOR AL ESTADO DE CUENTA         0000501301421012  
                 IND_CITY,      % INDICADOR DE ENVIO A CITYBANK         0000501401422012  
                 ENV_GCE,       % OPERACION RECIBIDA DE GCE             0000501501422022  
                 AFEC_CONTA;    % AFECTACION AL ESTADO DE CUENTA        0000501601423012  
                                                                        0000501701424012  
  EBCDIC ARRAY   REG[0:560], PAR_ENT[0:90],PAR_SAL[0:4249];             0000501801425012  
  BOOLEAN        BOOL_CAT;                                              0000501901426012  
                                                                        0000502001427012  
  HEX ARRAY      TEMP_ENT[0]=PAR_ENT,                                   0000502101428012  
                 TEMP_SAL[0]=PAR_SAL;                                   0000502201429012  
  POINTER        PTR_HEX,PTR_EBC;                                       0000502301430012  
                                                                        0000502401431012  
 PTR_HEX := TEMP_ENT[0];                                                0000502501432012  
 PTR_EBC := PAR_ENT[6];                                                 0000502601433012  
 REPLACE PTR_HEX:PTR_HEX     BY 000523200201 FOR 12 DIGITS;             0000502701434012  
 REPLACE PTR_EBC:PTR_EBC     BY "         ",                            0000502801435012  
                                SIST_LIB FOR 3 DIGITS,                  0000502901436012  
                                "000000000001",                         0000503001437012  
                                "            ",                         0000503101438012  
                                "            ",                         0000503201439012  
                                "            ",                         0000503301440012  
                                "            ";                         0000503401441012  
 RESULTADO := 0;                                                        0000503501442012  
 RESULTADO := L710_CONSUL_DETALLE(SIST_LIB,PROG,PAR_ENT,PAR_SAL);       0000503601443012  
 REPLACE REG BY TEMP_SAL[0] FOR 16 WITH HEXTOEBCDIC,"@" FOR 14,         0000503701444012  
         PAR_SAL[30] FOR 336,TEMP_SAL[732] FOR 24 WITH HEXTOEBCDIC,     0000503801445012  
         "@" FOR 4;                                                     0000503901446012  
 IF RESULTADO = 20 THEN                                                 0000504001447012  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000504101448012  
    REPLACE MSGLJ BY STR_HDRMSG, 48"00" FOR 227;                        0000504201449012  
    REPLACE TXTMSG BY "ERROR LIB/REGISTRAS",SIST_LIB FOR 3 DIGITS,      0000504301450012  
                      ", CATALOGO S080 VACIO, ",                        0000504401451012  
                      " NO EXISTE INFORMACION EN CAT. DE CATALOGOS";    0000504501452012  
    LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                                    0000504601453012  
    MYSELF.STATUS := -1;                                                0000504701454012  
 END                                                                    0000504801455012  
 ELSE                                                                   0000504901456012  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000505001457012  
    NUMCAT := IDFSAL_LLAVE(REG,4);                                      0000505101458012  
    FORMATO:= IDFSAL_LLAVE(REG,5);                                      0000505201459012  
 END;                                                                   0000505301460012  
 PTR_HEX := TEMP_ENT[0];                                                0000505401461012  
 PTR_EBC := PAR_ENT[6];                                                 0000505501462012  
 REPLACE PTR_HEX:PTR_HEX     BY NUMCAT FOR 6 DIGITS;                    0000505601463012  
 IF FORMATO = 2 THEN                                                    0000505701464012  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000505801465012  
    REPLACE PTR_HEX:PTR_HEX     BY 200101 FOR 6 DIGITS;                 0000505901466012

                                   SIST_LIB FOR 3 DIGITS,               0000506101468012  
                                   "            ";                      0000506201469012  
 END                                                                    0000506301470012  
 ELSE                                                                   0000506401471012  
 IF FORMATO = 1 THEN                                                    0000506501472012  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000506601473012  
    REPLACE PTR_HEX:PTR_HEX     BY 200201 FOR 6 DIGITS;                 0000506701474012  
    REPLACE PTR_EBC:PTR_EBC     BY "            ",                      0000506801475012  
                                   "        ",                          0000506901476012  
                                   SIST_LIB FOR 3 DIGITS;               0000507001477012

 ELSE                                                                   0000507201479012  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000507301480012  
    REPLACE MSGLJ BY STR_HDRMSG, 48"00" FOR 227;                        0000507401481012

                      ", CATALOGO S080 NUMERO ",                        0000507601483012  
                      " DE FORMATO NO CONTEMPLADO, FMTO = ",            0000507701484012  
                      FORMATO FOR 3 DIGITS;                             0000507801485012  
    LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                                    0000507901486012  
    MYSELF.STATUS := -1;                                                0000508001487012  
 END;                                                                   0000508101488012  
 REPLACE PTR_EBC:PTR_EBC     BY "            ",                         0000508201489012  
                                "            ",                         0000508301490012  
                                "            ",                         0000508401491012  
                                "            ";                         0000508501492012  
 PTR_HEX := TEMP_ENT[156];                                              0000508601493012  
 REPLACE PTR_HEX:PTR_HEX     BY 0000000 FOR 6 DIGITS,                   0000508701494012  
                                0000000 FOR 6 DIGITS,                   0000508801495012  
                                00      FOR 2 DIGITS;                   0000508901496012  
 SIST := 151;                                                           0000509001497012  
 RESULTADO := 0;                                                        0000509101498012  
 PROG := 012;                                                           0000509201499012

 REPLACE PAR_SAL[0] BY 4"0" FOR SIZE(PAR_SAL);                          0000509401501012  
 WHILE RESULTADO < 2 DO                                                 0000509501502012  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000509601503012  
    RESULTADO := L710_CONSUL_DETALLE(SIST,PROG,PAR_ENT,PAR_SAL);        0000509701504012  
    REPLACE REG BY TEMP_SAL[0] FOR 16 WITH HEXTOEBCDIC,"@" FOR 14,      0000509801505012  
            PAR_SAL[30] FOR 336,TEMP_SAL[732] FOR 24 WITH HEXTOEBCDIC,  0000509901506012  
            "@" FOR 4;                                                  0000510001507012  
    PTR_HEX := TEMP_ENT[162];                                           0000510101508012  
    PTR_EBC := PAR_ENT[6];                                              0000510201509012  
    FECINI  := IDFSALFECONS(REG);                                       0000510301510012  
    NIVINI  := IDFSALNIVINI(REG);                                       0000510401511012

      CLAVETR:=IDFSAL_LLAVE(REG,1)                                      0000510701514012  
    ELSE                                                                0000510801515012  
       CLAVETR:=IDFSAL_LLAVE(REG,2);                                    0000510901516012

                               DFSAL_LLAVE(REG,2) FOR 12,               0000511201519012  
                               DFSAL_LLAVE(REG,3) FOR 12,               0000511301520012  
                               DFSAL_LLAVE(REG,4) FOR 12,               0000511401521012  
                               DFSAL_LLAVE(REG,5) FOR 12,               0000511501522012  
                               DFSAL_LLAVE(REG,6) FOR 12;               0000511601523012  
    IF RESULTADO > 1 AND NOT BOOL_CAT THEN                              0000511701524012

    IF RESULTADO = 0 THEN                                               0000512601533012

       BOOL_CAT:=TRUE;                                                  0000512901536012  
    END                                                                 0000513001537012  
    ELSE                                                                0000513101538012  
    BEGIN                                                               0000513201539012

       ENV_GCE    := IDFSAL_LLAVE(REG,19);                              0000513601542112

       REPLACE ADF_IND_CITY(CLAVETR)  BY IND_CITY   FOR 2 DIGITS;       0000513901545012  
       REPLACE ADF_ENV_GCE (CLAVETR)  BY ENV_GCE    FOR 2 DIGITS;       0000514001545112  
    END;                                                                0000514101546012  
 END;                                                                   0000514201547012  
 END OF CARGA_CLAVES;                                                   0000514301548012

 PROCEDURE CLAVES_BNX_CITY;                                             0000514801553012  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000514901554012  
  INTEGER        SIST,          % SISTEMA                               0000515001555012  
                 PROG,          % PROGRAMA LLAMADOR                     0000515101556012

  EBCDIC ARRAY   REG[0:560], PAR_ENT[0:90],PAR_SAL[0:4249];             0000516201567012  
                                                                        0000516301568012  
  HEX ARRAY      TEMP_ENT[0]=PAR_ENT,                                   0000516401569012  
                 TEMP_SAL[0]=PAR_SAL;                                   0000516501570012  
  POINTER        PTR_HEX,PTR_EBC;                                       0000516601571012  
                                                                        0000516701572012  
 PTR_HEX := TEMP_ENT[0];                                                0000516801573012  
 PTR_EBC := PAR_ENT[6];                                                 0000516901574012  
 REPLACE PTR_HEX:PTR_HEX     BY 000807200201 FOR 12 DIGITS;             0000517001575012  
 REPLACE PTR_EBC:PTR_EBC     BY 0 FOR 12 DIGITS           ,             0000517101576012  
                                SIST_LIB FOR 12 DIGITS    ,             0000517201577012  
                                "            ",                         0000517301578012  
                                "            ",                         0000517401579012  
                                "            ",                         0000517501580012  
                                "            ";                         0000517601581012  
 PTR_HEX := TEMP_ENT[156];                                              0000517701582012  
 REPLACE PTR_HEX:PTR_HEX     BY 0000000 FOR 6 DIGITS,                   0000517801583012  
                                0000000 FOR 6 DIGITS,                   0000517901584012  
                                00      FOR 2 DIGITS;                   0000518001585012  
 RESULTADO := 0;                                                        0000518101586012  
 RESULTADO := L710_CONSUL_DETALLE(SIST_LIB,PROG,PAR_ENT,PAR_SAL);       0000518201587012  
 REPLACE REG BY TEMP_SAL[0] FOR 16 WITH HEXTOEBCDIC,"@" FOR 14,         0000518301588012  
         PAR_SAL[30] FOR 336,TEMP_SAL[732] FOR 24 WITH HEXTOEBCDIC,     0000518401589012  
         "@" FOR 4;                                                     0000518501590012  
 IF RESULTADO = 20 THEN                                                 0000518601591012  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000518701592012  
    REPLACE MSGLJ BY STR_HDRMSG, 48"00" FOR 227;                        0000518801593012  
    REPLACE TXTMSG BY "L012 > NO EXISTE INFORMACION EN CATALOGO ",      0000518901594012  
                      ", RELACIONAL DE CLAVES BNX - CITY (CAT 807) S",  0000519001595012  
                      SIST_LIB FOR 3 DIGITS;                            0000519101596012  
    LJ(CONLJ,MSGLJ);                                                    0000519201597012  
    MYSELF.STATUS := -1;                                                0000519301598012  
 END;                                                                   0000519401599012  
 SIST := 151;                                                           0000519501600012  
 PROG := 012;                                                           0000519601601012  
 REPLACE PAR_SAL[0] BY 4"0" FOR SIZE(PAR_SAL);                          0000519701602012  
 WHILE RESULTADO < 2  DO                                                0000519801603012  
 BEGIN                                                                  0000519901604012  
    CVE_BNX      := IDFSAL_LLAVE(REG,1);                                0000520001605012  
    CVE_CITY     := IDFSAL_LLAVE(REG,3);                                0000520101606012  
    CVE_CITY_REV := IDFSAL_LLAVE(REG,4);                                0000520201607012  
    REPLACE ADF_BNX_CITY  (CVE_BNX) BY CVE_CITY     FOR 4 DIGITS;       0000520301608012  
    REPLACE ADF_BNX_CB_REV(CVE_BNX) BY CVE_CITY_REV FOR 4 DIGITS;       0000520401609012  
    IF RESULTADO = 0 THEN                                               0000520501610012  
       RESULTADO := 3                                                   0000520601611012  
    ELSE                                                                0000520701612012  
    BEGIN                                                               0000520801613012  
       PTR_HEX := TEMP_ENT[162];                                        0000520901614012  
       PTR_EBC := PAR_ENT[6];                                           0000521001615012  
       FECINI  := IDFSALFECONS(REG);                                    0000521101616012  
       NIVINI  := IDFSALNIVINI(REG);                                    0000521201617012  
       REPLACE PTR_HEX:PTR_HEX BY FECINI FOR 6 DIGITS,                  0000521301618012  
                                  NIVINI FOR 2 DIGITS;                  0000521401619012  
       PTR_EBC := PAR_ENT[6];                                           0000521501620012  
       REPLACE PTR_EBC:PTR_EBC BY DFSAL_LLAVE(REG,1) FOR 12,            0000521601621012  
                                  DFSAL_LLAVE(REG,2) FOR 12,            0000521701622012  
                                  DFSAL_LLAVE(REG,3) FOR 12,            0000521801623012  
                                  DFSAL_LLAVE(REG,4) FOR 12,            0000521901624012  
                                  DFSAL_LLAVE(REG,5) FOR 12,            0000522001625012  
                                  DFSAL_LLAVE(REG,6) FOR 12;            0000522101626012  
       RESULTADO := L710_CONSUL_DETALLE(SIST,PROG,PAR_ENT,PAR_SAL);     0000522201627012  
       REPLACE REG BY TEMP_SAL[0] FOR 16 WITH HEXTOEBCDIC,              0000522301628012  
                      "@" FOR 14,                                       0000522401629012  
                      PAR_SAL[30] FOR 336,                              0000522501630012  
                      TEMP_SAL[732] FOR 24 WITH HEXTOEBCDIC,            0000522601631012  
                      "@" FOR 4;                                        0000522701632012

                       0  FOR 23 DIGITS,                                0000530901713012

    PROCEDURE ARMA_ID_942;                                              0000533801742012  
    BEGIN                                                               0000533901743012  
       DOUBLE NUMERO_BASE,   % BASE NUMBER                              0000534001744012  
              FORMATO_INT;   % INTERNAL FORMAT                          0000534101745012  
                                                                        0000534201746012  
 %===========================================================           0000534301746022  
 %      LLAMADO A CTAMEDIO   L016422 PARA OBTENER CTA GBL   =           0000534401746032  
 %===========================================================           0000534501746042  
        INTEGER PROCEDURE CTA_MEDIO(CTA_CVEPROD, CTA_CVEINS,            0000534601746052  
           CTA_CTANUM, CTA_SUCVTL, CTA_CTAVTL, CTA_NUMCTE, CTA_OPT);    0000534701746062  
        VALUE CTA_CVEPROD, CTA_CVEINS,                                  0000534801746072  
           CTA_CTANUM, CTA_SUCVTL, CTA_CTAVTL, CTA_NUMCTE, CTA_OPT;     0000534901746082  
        INTEGER CTA_CVEPROD, CTA_CVEINS, CTA_OPT, CTA_SUCVTL;           0000535001746092  
        DOUBLE CTA_CTANUM, CTA_CTAVTL, CTA_NUMCTE;                      0000535101746102  
        BEGIN                                                           0000535201746112  
          EBCDIC ARRAY ENTCTA_EB[0:280], SALCTA_EB[0:1499];             0000535301746122  
          ARRAY ENTCTA_RL[0:84], SALCTA_RL[0:84];                       0000535401746132  
          INTEGER RSTD_CTA;                                             0000535501746142  
                                                                        0000535601746152  
          REPLACE POINTER(ENTCTA_RL,4)                                  0000535701746162  
            BY CTA_CVEPROD           FOR 4 DIGITS,                      0000535801746172  
               CTA_CVEINS            FOR 4 DIGITS,                      0000535901746182  
               0                     FOR 4 DIGITS,                      0000536001746192  
               CTA_CTANUM            FOR 12 DIGITS,                     0000536101746202  
               1                     FOR 2 DIGITS,                      0000536201746212  
               CTA_OPT               FOR 2 DIGITS;                      0000536301746222  
                                                                        0000536401746232  
          REPLACE ENTCTA_EB BY ENTCTA_RL FOR 220;                       0000536501746242  
          RSTD_CTA := S016_L422_CTAMEDIO                                0000536601746252  
                      (ENTCTA_EB, SALCTA_EB);                           0000536701746262  
                                                                        0000536801746272  
          IF RSTD_CTA = 0 THEN                                          0000536901746282  
          BEGIN                                                         0000537001746292  
             REPLACE SALCTA_RL BY SALCTA_EB FOR 84;                     0000537101746302  
             REPLACE POINTER(ALOG_CDIR,4)+348 BY                        0000537201746304  
                CTAGBL_SUCMDA(SALCTA_RL) FOR 4 DIGITS;                  0000537301746306  
             REPLACE POINTER(ALOG_CDIR,4)+352 BY                        0000537401746308  
                CTAGBL_CTAMDA(SALCTA_RL) FOR 16 DIGITS;                 0000537501746310  
          END;                                                          0000537601746312  
          IF CTA_OPT = 0 THEN                                           0000537701746316  
          BEGIN                                                         0000537801746318  
            REPLACE MSGLJ BY STR_HDRMSG,                                0000537901746320  
                    " L422 S016 LLAMADO CTAMEDIO ",                     0000538001746322  
                    RSTD_CTA FOR 4 DIGITS         ,                     0000538101746324  
                    " OPCION 0 CANCELADA "        ,                     0000538201746326  
                    "  CTA VTL: ",  CTA_SUCVTL FOR 4 DIGITS,            0000538301746328  
                                    CTA_CTAVTL FOR 12 DIGITS,           0000538401746329  
                    "  NUM CTE: ",  CTA_NUMCTE FOR 12 DIGITS,           0000538501746330  
                    "  NUM PROD: ", CTA_CVEPROD FOR 4 DIGITS,           0000538601746331  
                    "  CVE INST: ", CTA_CVEINS FOR 4 DIGITS,            0000538701746332  
                    CRLF;                                               0000538801746333  
            WRITE(SALIDA,21,MSGLJ);                                     0000538901746334  
           END;                                                         0000539001746335  
          CTA_MEDIO := RSTD_CTA;                                        0000539101746342  
        END OF CTA_MEDIO;                                               0000539201746352

        PROCEDURE MDA_VIRTUAL(MDA_NUMCTE, MDA_SUCNUM,                   0000539701746402  
                              MDA_CTANUM);                              0000539801746407  
        VALUE MDA_NUMCTE, MDA_SUCNUM, MDA_CTANUM;                       0000539901746412  
        DOUBLE MDA_NUMCTE, MDA_CTANUM;                                  0000540001746422  
        INTEGER MDA_SUCNUM;                                             0000540101746427  
        BEGIN                                                           0000540201746432  
          EBCDIC ARRAY ENTMDA_EB[0:280], SALMDA_EB[0:1499];             0000540301746442  
          ARRAY ENTMDA_RL[0:84], SALMDA_RL[0:84];                       0000540401746452  
          INTEGER RSTD_MDA, RSTD_CTA, i;                                0000540501746462  
                                                                        0000540601746472  
          REPLACE POINTER(ENTMDA_RL,4) BY                               0000540701746482  
                    01                FOR 2  DIGITS,                    0000540801746492  
                    MDA_NUMCTE        FOR 12 DIGITS,                    0000540901746502  
                    0                 FOR 4  DIGITS,                    0000541001746512  
                    0                 FOR 2  DIGITS,                    0000541101746522  
                    0                 FOR 4  DIGITS,                    0000541201746532  
                    0                 FOR 12 DIGITS,                    0000541301746542  
                    " "               FOR 52,                           0000541401746552  
                    0                 FOR 2  DIGITS,                    0000541501746562  
                    MDA_SUCNUM        FOR 4  DIGITS,                    0000541601746567  
                    MDA_CTANUM        FOR 12 DIGITS;                    0000541701746572  
          REPLACE ENTMDA_EB BY ENTMDA_RL FOR 220;                       0000541801746582  
                                                                        0000541901746592  
          RSTD_MDA:=S016_L422_MDAVIRTUAL                                0000542001746602  
                    (ENTMDA_EB, SALMDA_EB);                             0000542101746612  
                                                                        0000542201746622  
          IF RSTD_MDA = 0 THEN                                          0000542301746632  
          BEGIN                                                         0000542401746642  
            REPLACE SALMDA_RL BY SALMDA_EB FOR 84;                      0000542501746652  
            i := 2;                                                     0000542601746662  
            RSTD_CTA := 1;                                              0000542701746672  
            WHILE RSTD_CTA NEQ 0 AND i > 0 DO                           0000542801746682  
            BEGIN                                                       0000542901746692  
             i := * - 1;                                                0000543001746702  
             RSTD_CTA := CTA_MEDIO                                      0000543101746712  
             (MDAVTL_CVEPRO(SALMDA_RL),                                 0000543201746722  
              MDAVTL_CVEINS(SALMDA_RL),                                 0000543301746732  
              MDAVTL_NUMCTA(SALMDA_RL),                                 0000543401746742  
              MDA_SUCNUM, MDA_CTANUM, MDA_NUMCTE, i);                   0000543501746747  
            END;                                                        0000543601746752  
          END                                                           0000543701746762  
          ELSE                                                          0000543801746772  
          BEGIN                                                         0000543901746782  
             REPLACE MSGLJ BY STR_HDRMSG,                               0000544001746792  
                     " L422 S016 LLAMADO ERROR #",                      0000544101746802  
                     RSTD_CTA FOR 4 DIGITS       ,                      0000544201746812  
                     " ENTRY POINT MDAVIRTUAL,"  ,                      0000544301746822  
                     "  CTA VTL: ", MDA_SUCNUM FOR 4 DIGITS,            0000544401746825  
                                    MDA_CTANUM FOR 12 DIGITS,           0000544501746829  
                     "  NUM CTE: ", MDA_NUMCTE FOR 12 DIGITS,           0000544601746832  
                     CRLF;                                              0000544701746842  
             WRITE(SALIDA,16,MSGLJ);                                    0000544801746852  
          END;                                                          0000544901746862  
        END OF MDA_VIRTUAL;                                             0000545001746872  
                                                                        0000545101746882  
%===========================================================            0000545201747012  
%        CONVERSION DE CUENTA BANAMEX A CUENTA CITY        =            0000545301748012  
%===========================================================            0000545401749012  
                                                                        0000545501750012  
       PROCEDURE CUENTAS_CITY(ISUC,CUENTA,BASE_CITY,FMTO_INTERNO);      0000545601751012  
       VALUE ISUC,CUENTA;                                               0000545701752012  
       REFERENCE BASE_CITY,FMTO_INTERNO;                                0000545801753012  
       INTEGER ISUC;                                                    0000545901754012  
       DOUBLE  CUENTA,BASE_CITY,FMTO_INTERNO;                           0000546001755012  
       BEGIN                                                            0000546101756012  
           EBCDIC ARRAY ARR_CTA, ARR_SAL[0:17];                         0000546201757012  
                                                                        0000546301758012  
           REPLACE ARR_CTA BY ISUC   FOR 4 DIGITS,                      0000546401759012  
                              CUENTA FOR 7 DIGITS;                      0000546501760012  
           BASE_CITY := DOUBLE(ARR_CTA[2],6);                           0000546601761012  
           REPLACE ARR_SAL BY                                           0000546701762012  
                   INTEGER(ARR_CTA[0], 1)     FOR 1 DIGITS,             0000546801763012  
                   INTEGER(ARR_CTA[2], 6)     FOR 6 DIGITS,             0000546901764012  
                   INTEGER(ARR_CTA[1], 1)     FOR 1 DIGITS,             0000547001765012  
                   INTEGER(ARR_CTA[8], 3)     FOR 3 DIGITS;             0000547101766012  
           FMTO_INTERNO := DOUBLE(ARR_SAL[0],11);                       0000547201767012  
       END OF CUENTAS_CITY;                                             0000547301768012  
                                                                        0000547401769012  
       TRANSFIERE := TRUE;                                              0000547501770012  
       IF IDFCTAMDA(ALOG_CDIR) = 0 THEN                                 0000547601771012  
       BEGIN                                                            0000547701772012  
          REPLACE POINTER(ENTRADA_S016,4)                               0000547801773012  
               BY IDFPRODUCT(ALOG_CDIR) FOR 4 DIGITS,                   0000547901774012  
                  IDFCVEINST(ALOG_CDIR) FOR 4 DIGITS,                   0000548001775012  
                  IDFSUCPROM(ALOG_CDIR) FOR 4 DIGITS,                   0000548101776012  
                  IDFCUENTA (ALOG_CDIR) FOR 12 DIGITS,                  0000548201777012  
                  1                     FOR 2 DIGITS,                   0000548301778012  
                  0                     FOR 2 DIGITS,                   0000548401779012  
                  CSI_LIB               FOR 2 DIGITS,                   0000548501780012  
                  0                     FOR 12 DIGITS;                  0000548601781012  
          REPLACE ENT_S016 BY ENTRADA_S016 FOR 40 ;                     0000548701782012  
          RESP_S016 := S016_L422_CTAMEDIO(ENT_S016,SAL_S016);           0000548801783012  
          IF RESP_S016 = 0 THEN                                         0000548901784012  
          BEGIN                                                         0000549001785012  
             REPLACE SALIDA_S016 BY SAL_S016 FOR 84;                    0000549101786012  
             PREF_MDA    := IS422_SMDA  (SALIDA_S016);                  0000549201787012  
             CUENTA_MDA  := IS422_CTAMDA(SALIDA_S016);                  0000549301788012  
             CUENTAS_CITY(PREF_MDA    ,                                 0000549401789012  
                          CUENTA_MDA  ,                                 0000549501790012  
                          NUMERO_BASE ,                                 0000549601791012  
                          FORMATO_INT);                                 0000549701792012  
          END                                                           0000549801793012  
          ELSE                                                          0000549901794012  
          BEGIN                                                         0000550001795012  
             NUMERO_BASE := 0;                                          0000550101796012  
             FORMATO_INT := 0;                                          0000550201797012  
          END                                                           0000550301798012  
       END                                                              0000550401799012  
       ELSE                                                             0000550501800012  
          CUENTAS_CITY(IDFSUCMDA(ALOG_CDIR),                            0000550601801012  
                       IDFCTAMDA(ALOG_CDIR),                            0000550701802012  
                       NUMERO_BASE,                                     0000550801803012  
                       FORMATO_INT);                                    0000550901804012  
      CTA_VIRTUAL_CTA := CTA_VIRTUAL_SUC := 0;                          0000551001804032  
      CTA_GLOBAL := FALSE;                                              0000551101804062  
      IF A = 1 AND IDFSUCMDA(ALOG_CDIR)>=6502                           0000551201804112  
               AND IDFSUCMDA(ALOG_CDIR)<=6541 THEN                      0000551301804212  
      BEGIN                                                             0000551401804312  
             CTA_VIRTUAL_CTA:=IDFCTAMDA(ALOG_CDIR);                     0000551501804412  
             CTA_VIRTUAL_SUC:=IDFSUCMDA(ALOG_CDIR);                     0000551601804422  
             CTA_GLOBAL := TRUE;                                        0000551701804462  
             MDA_VIRTUAL(IDFCLIENTE(ALOG_CDIR),                         0000551801804512  
                         IDFSUCMDA(ALOG_CDIR),                          0000551901804562  
                         IDFCTAMDA(ALOG_CDIR));                         0000552001804612  
      END;                                                              0000552101804712  
       LONGITUD_PARCIAL := 0;                                           0000552201805012  
       IF NOT REENVIA_MOVTOS THEN                                       0000552301806012  
          NUMERO_ARCHIVO := * + 1;                                      0000552401807012  
       REPLACE ARR_942 BY 48"00" FOR 1920;                              0000552501808012  
       PTR1_942 := POINTER(ARR_942);                                    0000552601809012  
%===== PRISM header                                                     0000552701810012  
       REPLACE PTR1_942:PTR1_942 BY                                     0000552801811012  
                  ":PMT:MT942",         %Message type      %09          0000552901812012  
                  CRLF,                                                 0000553001813012  
                  ":PAI:",              %Account ID        %05          0000553101814012  
                  IDFSUCMDA  (ALOG_CDIR) FOR 04 DIGITS,    %04          0000553201815012  
                  IDFCTAMDA  (ALOG_CDIR) FOR 07 DIGITS,    %07          0000553301816012  
                  CRLF,                                                 0000553401817012  
                  ":PCI:",              %Local Customer ID %05          0000553501818012  
                  IDFCLIENTE (ALOG_CDIR) FOR 12 DIGITS,    %12          0000553601819012  
                  CRLF,                                                 0000553701820012  
                  ":POI:BANAMEX",       %Originator ID     %12          0000553801821012  
                  CRLF,                                                 0000553901822012  
                  ":POR:",        %Orig. Reference Number  %05          0000554001823012  
                  0                      FOR  2 DIGITS,    %2           0000554101824012  
                  IDFAUTAPLI (ALOG_CDIR) FOR  8 DIGITS,    %08          0000554201825012  
                  IDFSISORI  (ALOG_CDIR) FOR  4 DIGITS,    %04          0000554301826012  
                  IDFCSIORI  (ALOG_CDIR) FOR  2 DIGITS,    %02          0000554401827012  
                  CRLF,                                                 0000554501828012  
                  ":POT:",  % Originator Time              %05          0000554601829012  
                  IDFFECOPE  (ALOG_CDIR) FOR  8 DIGITS,    %08          0000554701830012  
                  IDFHORAOPE (ALOG_CDIR) FOR  6 DIGITS,    %06          0000554801831012  
                  CRLF,                                                 0000554901832012  
                  ":PPD:";  %Posible Duplicate Flag       %06           0000555001833012  
            IF REENVIA_MOVTOS OR REENVIO_POR_HI THEN                    0000555101834012  
               REPLACE PTR1_942 : PTR1_942 BY "Y"          %01          0000555201835012  
            ELSE                                                        0000555301836012  
               REPLACE PTR1_942 : PTR1_942 BY " ";                      0000555401837012  
                                                                        0000555501838012  
            REPLACE PTR1_942 : PTR1_942 BY                              0000555601839012  
                  CRLF,                                                 0000555701840012  
                  ":PPL:485  ", %Processing Location        %09         0000555801841012  
                  CRLF,                                                 0000555901842012  
                  ":PND:",      %End of Header Tag         %05          0000556001843012  
                  CRLF;                                                 0000556101844012  
%===== TAG 20                                                           0000556201845012  
       REPLACE PTR1_942:PTR1_942 BY                                     0000556301846012  
                  ":20:";                                               0000556401847012  
  LONG_STR1 := 16; PUNTO_INICIO := 231;                                 0000556501848012  
  WHILE PUNTO_INICIO > 215 AND LETRA(ALOG_CDIR,PUNTO_INICIO) = " " DO   0000556601849012  
  BEGIN                                                                 0000556701850012  
        PUNTO_INICIO := PUNTO_INICIO -1;                                0000556801851012  
        LONG_STR1: = LONG_STR1 - 1;                                     0000556901852012  
  END;                                                                  0000557001853012  
       IF LONG_STR1 = 0 THEN                                            0000557101854012  
       BEGIN                                                            0000557201855012  
          REPLACE PTR1_942:PTR1_942 BY                                  0000557301856012  
                  IDFSUCTRAN(ALOG_CDIR) FOR 4 DIGITS,   % 4             0000557401857012  
                  IDFAUTAPLI(ALOG_CDIR) FOR 12 DIGITS;                  0000557501858012  
       END                                                              0000557601859012  
       ELSE                                                             0000557701860012  
          REPLACE PTR1_942:PTR1_942                                     0000557801861012  
              BY  POINTER(ALOG_CDIR)+216 FOR 16;                        0000557901862012
