      PROCEDURE ANALIZA(TAMANO,CAMPO_ALFA,CAMPO_NUM,CAMPO_STR,TIPO);    0000586402217082  
      REFERENCE TAMANO, CAMPO_ALFA, CAMPO_NUM, CAMPO_STR, TIPO;         0000586502217092  
      DOUBLE       CAMPO_NUM     ;                                      0000586602217102  
      ARRAY        CAMPO_ALFA[*] ;                                      0000586702217112  
      STRING       CAMPO_STR     ;                                      0000586802217122  
      BOOLEAN      TIPO          ;                                      0000586902217132  
      INTEGER      TAMANO        ;                                      0000587002217142  
      BEGIN                                                             0000587102217152  
         INTEGER   LONG_DATO, CONT_NUMEROS;                             0000587202217162  
         STRING    STR_CEROS;                                           0000587302217172  
         TIPO      := FALSE;                                            0000587402217174  
         CAMPO_STR := STRING(CAMPO_ALFA,TAMANO);                        0000587502217182  
         CAMPO_STR := HEAD(TAIL(CAMPO_STR," "),NOT " ");                0000587602217192  
         TAMANO    := LENGTH(CAMPO_STR);                                0000587702217202  
         REPLACE POINTER(CAMPO_ALFA) BY " " FOR SIZE(CAMPO_ALFA);       0000587802217212  
         REPLACE POINTER(CAMPO_ALFA) BY CAMPO_STR;                      0000587902217222  
                                                                        0000588002217232  
         SCAN POINTER(CAMPO_ALFA) FOR CONT_NUMEROS:TAMANO               0000588102217242  
                                  WHILE IN NUMEROS;                     0000588202217252  
         CONT_NUMEROS := TAMANO - CONT_NUMEROS;                         0000588302217262  
         TAMANO    := LENGTH(CAMPO_STR);                                0000588402217272  
         IF CONT_NUMEROS = TAMANO THEN                                  0000588502217282  
         BEGIN                                                          0000588602217292  
            TIPO      := TRUE ;                                         0000588702217302  
            STR_CEROS := HEAD(CAMPO_STR,"0");                           0000588802217312  
            IF LENGTH(STR_CEROS) < TAMANO THEN                          0000588902217322  
            BEGIN                                                       0000589002217332  
               CAMPO_STR := DROP(CAMPO_STR, LENGTH(STR_CEROS));         0000589102217342  
               CAMPO_NUM := DECIMAL(CAMPO_STR);                         0000589202217352  
               TAMANO    := LENGTH(CAMPO_STR);                          0000589302217362  
            END                                                         0000589402217372  
            ELSE                                                        0000589502217382  
            BEGIN                                                       0000589602217392  
               TAMANO    := 1;                                          0000589702217402  
               CAMPO_STR := "0";                                        0000589802217412  
               CAMPO_NUM := 0                                           0000589902217422  
            END;                                                        0000590002217432  
         END                                                            0000590102217442  
         ELSE                                                           0000590202217452  
         BEGIN                                                          0000590302217472  
            CAMPO_NUM := 0;                                             0000590402217482  
            SCAN POINTER(CAMPO_ALFA) FOR CONT_NUMEROS:TAMANO            0000590502217484  
                                     WHILE > 48"00";                    0000590602217486  
            TAMANO := TAMANO - CONT_NUMEROS;                            0000590702217488  
            IF TAMANO = 0 THEN CAMPO_STR := 48"00";                     0000590802217490  
         END;                                                           0000590902217496  
      END; %FIN DE PROCEDURE ANALIZA                                    0000591002217502

            BEGIN                                                       0000609802252012  
                 PUNTO_INICIO := PUNTO_INICIO -1;                       0000609902253012  
                 LONG_STR1: = LONG_STR1 - 1;                            0000610002254012  
            END;                                                        0000610102255012  
            IF LONG_STR1 = 0 THEN                                       0000610202256012  
            BEGIN                                                       0000610302257012  
               REPLACE PTR1_942:PTR1_942 BY                             0000610402258012  
                       "0000"                           ,               0000610502258112  
                       IDFSUCTRAN(ALOG_CDIR) FOR 4 DIGITS,   % 4        0000610602259012  
                       IDFAUTAPLI(ALOG_CDIR) FOR 8 DIGITS               0000610702260012  
            END                                                         0000610802261012  
            ELSE                                                        0000610902262012  
               REPLACE PTR1_942:PTR1_942 BY                             0000611002263012  
                       POINTER(ALOG_CDIR)+216 FOR 16;                   0000611102264012  
                                                                        0000611202264112  
       IF IDFSTATUS(ALOG_CDIR) = 1 THEN                                 0000611302264212  
          CLAVE_CITY := IADF_BNX_CITY(CLAVE_BNX)                        0000611402264232  
       ELSE                                                             0000611502264252  
          CLAVE_CITY := IADF_BNX_CB_REV(CLAVE_BNX);                     0000611602264272  
                                                                        0000611702264292  
       REPLACE PTR1_942:PTR1_942 BY CRLF                  ,             0000611802264312  
                                    "/CTC/"               ,             0000611902264412  
                                    CLAVE_CITY FOR 3 DIGITS;            0000612002265012  
%===== SE OBTIENE NUEVAMENTE LA REF NUMERICA NORMALIZADA PARA           0000612102265032  
%===== QUE PUEDA SER UTILIZADA EN EL TAG 86                             0000612202265052  
       REF_STR := REPEAT(" ",16);                                       0000612302265112  
       REPLACE EBC_REF_ALFA[0]   BY IDFREFMOV(ALOG_CDIR) FOR 16 DIGITS; 0000612402265212  
       REPLACE POINTER(REF_ALFA) BY EBC_REF_ALFA         FOR 16;        0000612502265312  
       NUM_CARACTERES := 16;                                            0000612602265412  
       ANALIZA(NUM_CARACTERES,REF_ALFA,REF_NUMERICA,REF_STR,NUMERICO);  0000612702265512  
       ARMA_TAG86S;                                                     0000612802266012  
                                                                        0000612902266112  
   END OF ARMA_TAG61;                                                   0000613002267012

    PROCEDURE ARMA_DET_942;                                             0000613402271012  
    BEGIN                                                               0000613502272012  
       IF IDFMONEDA(ALOG_CDIR) = 1 THEN                                 0000613602273012  
          REPLACE STR_MONEDA BY "MXN"                                   0000613702274012  
       ELSE                                                             0000613802275012  
          REPLACE STR_MONEDA BY "USD";                                  0000613902276012  
       FECHA_VAL := IDFFECVAL(ALOG_CDIR);                               0000614002277012  
       IF FECHA_VAL = 0 THEN                                            0000614102277112  
          FECHA_VAL := IDFECONT(ALOG_CDIR);                             0000614202277212  
       REPLACE MMDD BY IDFMMDDCON(ALOG_CDIR) FOR 4 DIGITS;              0000614302278012  
       MMDD_VAL := INTEGER(MMDD,4);                                     0000614402279012  
                                                                        0000614502280012  
       ARMA_ID_942;                                                     0000614602281012  
                                                                        0000614702282012  
       IF IDFSTATUS(ALOG_CDIR) = 1 THEN                                 0000614802283012  
          IF CARGO THEN               %CARGO = DEBITO                   0000614902284012  
          BEGIN                                                         0000615002290012  
             REPLACE IDTIPO_MOVCONT BY "DR";                            0000615102291012  
             IDLONG := 2;                                               0000615202292012  
             ARMA_TAG61;                                                0000615302293012  
          END                                                           0000615402294012  
          ELSE                %ABONO = CREDITO                          0000615502295012  
          BEGIN                                                         0000615602296012  
             REPLACE IDTIPO_MOVCONT BY "CR";                            0000615702297012  
             IDLONG := 2;                                               0000615802298012  
             ARMA_TAG61;                                                0000615902299012  
          END                                                           0000616002300012  
       ELSE     %========= ELIMINACIONES =============                  0000616102301012  
          IF CARGO THEN       %ELIMINACION CARGO                        0000616202302012  
          BEGIN                                                         0000616302308012  
             REPLACE IDTIPO_MOVCONT BY "RD";                            0000616402309012  
             IDLONG := 2;                                               0000616502310012  
             ARMA_TAG61;                                                0000616602311012  
          END                                                           0000616702312012  
          ELSE                 %ELIMINACION ABONO                       0000616802313012  
          BEGIN                                                         0000616902314012  
             REPLACE IDTIPO_MOVCONT BY "RC";                            0000617002315012  
             IDLONG := 2;                                               0000617102316012  
             ARMA_TAG61;                                                0000617202317012  
          END;                                                          0000617302318012  
    END OF ARMA_DET_942;                                                0000617402319012

    PROCEDURE ARMA_HEADER(REGISTRO);                                    0000617602321012  
    ARRAY REGISTRO[*];                                                  0000617702322012  
    BEGIN                                                               0000617802323012  
      IF REENVIA_MOVTOS OR REENVIO_POR_HI THEN                          0000617902324012  
         CLASE_MSG := 1                                                 0000618002325012  
      ELSE                                                              0000618102326012  
         CLASE_MSG := 0;                                                0000618202327012  
                                                                        0000618302328012  
      REPLACE IDFHORAENV BY TIME(9) FOR 6;                              0000618402329012  
      REPLACE HDR_PREFIJO(EBC_MSGIN_BNX)                                0000618502330012  
           BY "F"     ,                    %PREFIJO                     0000618602331012  
              "0"     ,                    %APLICACION DESTINO          0000618702332012  
               CSI_DEST FOR 2 DIGITS,      %CSI DESTINO                 0000618802333012  
               0 FOR 4 DIGITS,             %NUMERO DE ESTACION          0000618902334012  
               " "            ,            %ESTACION TIPO               0000619002335012  
               0 FOR 4 DIGITS ,            %SUC NUMERO                  0000619102336012  
               " "            ,            %PRIV. DE LA TERM.           0000619202337012  
               0 FOR 2 DIGITS ,            %NUMERO CAJA                 0000619302338012  
               CSI_LIB FOR 2 DIGITS,       %CSI ORIGEN                  0000619402339012  
               CSI_LIB FOR 2 DIGITS,       %NODO ORIGEN                 0000619502340012  
               0 FOR 2 DIGITS ,            %FACULTAD DEL OPE.           0000619602341012  
               0 FOR 1 DIGITS ,            %PREF. APLIC. ORIGEN         0000619702342012  
               2 FOR 2 DIGITS ,            %RESULTADO                   0000619802343012  
               " " FOR 5      ,            %NOMBRE DE MASCARA           0000619902344012  
               0 FOR 6 DIGITS ,            %CVE SEG. DEL OPER.          0000620002345012  
               13701 FOR 6 DIGITS,         %COD APL DESTINO             0000620102346012  
               15112 FOR 6 DIGITS,         %COD APL ORIGEN              0000620202347012  
%SEGMENTO DE LA INFRAESTRUCTURA                                         0000620302348012  
               0137  FOR 4 DIGITS,         %SISTEMA HOST                0000620402349012  
               01    FOR 2 DIGITS,         %CODIGO DEL SISTEMA          0000620502350012  
               001   FOR 3 DIGITS,         %SUBCODIGO DE SERVICIO       0000620602351012  
               0151  FOR 4 DIGITS,         %SISTEMA CLIENTE             0000620702352012  
               12    FOR 2 DIGITS,         %CODIGO DE SERVICIO          0000620802353012  
               001   FOR 3 DIGITS,         %SUBCODIGO DE SERVICIO       0000620902354012  
               1     FOR 1 DIGITS,         %DIRECCION                   0000621002355012  
               0     FOR 4 DIGITS,         %LONGITUD DEL MENSAJE        0000621102356012  
               00060 FOR 5 DIGITS,         %TIEMPO DE ESPERA            0000621202357012  
               01    FOR 2 DIGITS,         %RESULTADO DE ICA            0000621302358012  
               00    FOR 2 DIGITS,         %IDENTIFICACION DEL FORMATO  0000621402359012  
               0     FOR 1 DIGITS,         %RECONOCIMIENTO DEL MENSAJE  0000621502360012  
               "B"              ,          %VERSION DEL FORMATO         0000621602361012  
               CLASE_MSG FOR 1 DIGITS,     %CLASE DE MENSAJE            0000621702362012  
               0         FOR 1 DIGITS,     %RESULTADO                   0000621802363012  
               1         FOR 1 DIGITS,     %MODO INTERACTIVO            0000621902364012  
               0         FOR 4 DIGITS,     %ENTIDAD ORIGEN              0000622002365012  
               "0"       FOR 3       ,     %CAJA ORIGEN                 0000622102366012  
          NMSG_DESGLOSADO  FOR 8 DIGITS,    %NUMERO DE TRANSACCION      0000622202367012  
       FECHA_MAQ_6D      FOR 6 DIGITS,     %FECHA ENVIO DE TRANSACC.    0000622302368012  
 INTEGER(POINTER(IDFHORAENV),6)  FOR 6 DIGITS, %HORA ENVIO DE TRANSAC.  0000622402369012  
               01        FOR 2 DIGITS,    %MODALIDAD                    0000622502370012  
               " "       FOR 10,           %IDETIFICADOR DEL OPERADOR   0000622602371012  
               "B"                    , %VERSION DE DE DATOS OPCIONALES 0000622702372012  
               POINTER(REGISTRO) FOR LEN_REG,                           0000622802373012  
               CRLF,                                                    0000622902374012  
               "-"," " FOR 70,                                          0000623002375012  
               CRLF,                                                    0000623102376012  
               48"00";                                                  0000623202377012  
    END OF ARMA_HEADER;                                                 0000623302378012  
                                                                        0000623402379012  
    INTEGER PROCEDURE CREA_BIFIN;                                       0000623502380012  
    BEGIN                                                               0000623602381012  
                                                                        0000623702381112  
        CREA_BIFIN := 0;                                                0000623802381212  
        REPLACE IDFHORAENV BY TIME(9) FOR 6;                            0000623902382012  
        BHORA := INTEGER(POINTER(IDFHORAENV),6);                        0000624002383012  
        FIND       B04SXSISTFEC AT                                      0000624102386012  
                   B04-CID-SISTEMA     = SIST_LIB      AND              0000624202387012  
                   B04-CID-FECENV      = FECHA_PROCESO AND              0000624302388012  
                   B04-CID-CTLFILE     = NREG_FISICO   AND              0000624402389012  
                   B04-CID-CTLENVIO    = NMSG_DESGLOSADO AND            0000624502390012  
                   B04-CID-AUTASIENTO  = INDICE        AND              0000624602391012  
                   B04-CID-AUTS151     = IDFAUT(ALOG_CDIR) :DMSTATUS;   0000624702392012  
        IF BOOLEAN(DMSTATUS) THEN                                       0000624802393012  
           IF DMSTATUS.DMERROR    = NOTFOUND THEN                       0000624902394012  
           BEGIN                                                        0000625002395012  
              REPLACE AUTORIZ_NIO[0] BY DFAUTNIO(ALOG_CDIR) FOR 16;     0000625102396012  
              CREATE S151B04CTLCITIDIR;                                 0000625202397012  
              MI_MIX := SNR;                                            0000625302397112  
              PUT S151B04CTLCITIDIR(                                    0000625402399012  
                  B04-CID-SISTEMA := SIST_LIB         ,                 0000625502400012  
                  B04-CID-FECENV  := FECHA_PROCESO    ,                 0000625602401012  
                  B04-CID-CTLFILE := NREG_FISICO      ,                 0000625702402012  
                  B04-CID-CTLENVIO:= NMSG_DESGLOSADO  ,                 0000625802403012  
                  B04-CID-AUTS151 := IDFAUT(ALOG_CDIR),                 0000625902404012  
                  B04-CID-AUTASIENTO := INDICE        ,                 0000626002405012  
                  B04-CID-HORENV  := BHORA            ,                 0000626102406012  
                  B04-CID-NIO     := AUTORIZ_NIO[0]   ,                 0000626202407012  
                  B04-CID-ESTATUS := 0                ,                 0000626302408012  
                  B04-CID-REINTENTOS := 0)            ;                 0000626402409012  
              BEGINTRANSACTION NOAUDIT S151B99REINICTL :DMSTATUS;       0000626502410012  
              IF NOT BOOLEAN(DMSTATUS) THEN                             0000626602410112  
              BEGIN                                                     0000626702410212  
                 STORE S151B04CTLCITIDIR : DMSTATUS;                    0000626802410312  
                 IF BOOLEAN(DMSTATUS) THEN                              0000626902410412  
                 BEGIN                                                  0000627002410512  
                    REPLACE MSG_ERR BY STR_HDRLOG, 48"00" FOR 227;      0000627102410532  
                    REPLACE MSG_ERR[23] BY "ERR CREAT 01 CAT. "      ,  0000627202410552  
                                        DMSTATUS.DMERROR FOR 2 DIGITS,  0000627302410556  
                                        " ERROR "                    ,  0000627402410558  
                                        DMSTATUS.DMERRORTYPE            0000627502410560  
                                        FOR 4 DIGITS                 ,  0000627602410562  
                                        " # MSG "                    ,  0000627702410564  
                                        NMSG_DESGLOSADO FOR 6 DIGITS ;  0000627802410566  
                    WRITE(SALIDA,40,MSG_ERR);                           0000627902410582  
                    CREA_BIFIN := 1;                                    0000628002410592  
                 END;                                                   0000628102410602  
                 ENDTRANSACTION AUDIT S151B99REINICTL;                  0000628202410612  
              END                                                       0000628302410712  
              ELSE                                                      0000628402410812  
                 IF DMSTATUS.DMERROR = AUDITERROR AND                   0000628502410912  
                    DMSTATUS.DMERRORTYPE = 1 THEN                       0000628602411012  
                 BEGIN                                                  0000628702411112  
                    STORE S151B04CTLCITIDIR : DMSTATUS;                 0000628802411212  
                    IF BOOLEAN(DMSTATUS) THEN                           0000628902411312  
                    BEGIN                                               0000629002411412  
                       REPLACE MSG_ERR BY STR_HDRLOG, 48"00" FOR 227;   0000629102411432  
                       REPLACE MSG_ERR[23] BY "ERR CREAT 02 CAT. "     ,0000629202411452  
                                          DMSTATUS.DMERROR FOR 2 DIGITS,0000629302411456  
                                          " ERROR "                    ,0000629402411458  
                                          DMSTATUS.DMERRORTYPE          0000629502411460  
                                          FOR 4 DIGITS                 ,0000629602411462  
                                          " # MSG "                    ,0000629702411464  
                                          NMSG_DESGLOSADO FOR 6 DIGITS ;0000629802411466  
                       WRITE(SALIDA,40,MSG_ERR);                        0000629902411482  
                       CREA_BIFIN := 1         ;                        0000630002411492  
                    END;                                                0000630102411502  
                 END;                                                   0000630202411512  
              FREE   S151B04CTLCITIDIR :DMSTATUS;                       0000630302414112  
           END;                                                         0000630402415012  
    END OF CREA_BIFIN;                                                  0000630502416012

     PROCEDURE ENVIA_MOVIMIENTOS;                                       0000630702436012

                 IADF_IND_CITY(CLAVE_BNX) = 2 THEN                      0000633302461012

                 AFC_CONTA  := IADF_AFCCONTA(CLAVE_BNX);                0000633602464012

                 IF VALIDA_CVE(CLAVE_BNX,INDEDO_CTA, AFC_CONTA) THEN    0000633802466012  
                 BEGIN                                                  0000633902467012  
                    IF IADF_AFCCONTA(CLAVE_BNX)  = 1 OR                 0000634002468012  
                       CLAVE_BNX = 2005              OR                 0000634102468112  
                       CLAVE_BNX = 2006              OR                 0000634202468212  
                       CLAVE_BNX = 2007              THEN               0000634302468222  
                       CARGO := TRUE;                                   0000634402469012  
                    D_ENTERO := IDFENT_OCUR(ALOG_CDIR,INDICE);          0000634502470012

                    ARMA_DET_942;                                       0000634802473012

                 END;                                                   0000635002474012  
              END;                                                      0000635102475012  
                                                                        0000635202476012  
           IF TRANSFIERE THEN                                           0000635302477012  
           BEGIN                                                        0000635402478012

                   ARMA_HEADER(ARR_942);                                0000636802492012  
                 END;                                                   0000636902493012  
             OBTEN_EVE(MI_MIX,1);                                       0000637002494012  
             WHILE CREA_BIFIN > 0 DO WAIT((.01));                       0000637102495012

             WRITE(SALIDA,150,TEXTO_TAGS);                              0000638502502612

             LEN_REG := 3000 - LEN_REG;                                 0000638702504012  
             REPLACE HDR_LEN_MSG(EBC_MSGIN_BNX) BY LEN_REG FOR 4 DIGITS;0000638802505012  
             RESULT_ENVIO_TCP := 0;                                     0000638902508012

                   RESULT_ENVIO_TCP := * + 10                           0000639802511142

                   RESULT_ENVIO_TCP := 99;                              0000640002513162  
                MI_MIX := SNR;                                          0000640102513167  
                OBTEN_EVE(MI_MIX,2);                                    0000640202513172  
                ACTZA_BIFIN(0,                                          0000640302513182  
                            RESULT_ENVIO_TCP  ,                         0000640402513192  
                            FECHA_PROCESO     ,                         0000640502513212

                                                                        0000640902516012

             BEGIN                                                      0000641102518012

                    NOT LIB_SBC            THEN                         0000642802527112

              END                                                       0000643302533012  
           END;  % FIN DE IF TRANSFIERE THEN                            0000643402534012  
           INDICE := INDICE + 1;                                        0000643502535012  
           CLAVE_BNX := IDFCVEOCUR(ALOG_CDIR, INDICE);                  0000643602536112  
         END; %FIN DE WHILE INDICE < 6 AND CLAVE_BNX > 0 DO             0000643702537012  
        END;     % FIN DE IF IDFTIPPROC(ALOG_CDIR)  < 16  AND           0000643802538012  
                 %           IDFFUNCION(ALOG_CDIR) = 1   THEN           0000643902539012

           (IDFEVIOCD(ALOG_CDIR) = 2 OR IDFEVIOCD(ALOG_CDIR) = 3) THEN  0000644702547512
